# Continuity Ledger

This append-only ledger lets a new agent recover the exact operational state without relying on any prior chat. It is not a scientific-result log and cannot authorize Task 4F1.

## Update rule

For every load-bearing stage transition, append one entry before handing work off. Do not edit or reorder prior entries. A transition is incomplete unless the entry names its predecessor anchor, changed paths, verification evidence, explicit status, hard stops, and exactly one next action.

## Entry schema

```text
L-NNN
timestamp_utc:
actor_role:
predecessor_commit_or_tag:
scope:
changed_or_created_paths:
verification:
outcome_boundary:
status:
next_single_action:
handoff_payload:
```

blocking_finding: Gate 3. The pinned 100K::12 representation canary does not reproduce in a fully lock-conformant environment. governance_note: The V3 audit prompt forbids the auditor from committing or pushing, so audit outputs were produced without commit inside the auditor namespace and then carried by the Head Researcher role onto an audit/... branch per CHAIN_OF_CUSTODY rule 2. Disclosure: in this session one agent held both the Head Researcher/Continuity Lead and independent-auditor roles. The auditor did not implement V3 (a prior agent did) and accepted no prior chat, narrative or claimed verdict as evidence, re-deriving every declared hash and behaviour independently; a successor may nevertheless commission a second independent audit of these same bytes if stricter role separation is required.

### L-007

```text
timestamp_utc: 2026-09-01T16:00:00Z
actor_role: Head Researcher / Continuity Lead

changed_or_created_paths: ops/CURRENT_STATE.json; docs/CONTINUITY_LEDGER.md
decision: The V4 audit package is to be persisted on a NEW branch audit/v52-t4f1-v4-independent-2026-09-01. This follows CHAIN_OF_CUSTODY rule 2 and the V3 precedent (audit/v52-t4f1-v3-independent-2026-09-01 at a590f629). Committing audit output to main is refused: main is canonical ACCEPTED state, and landing an unreviewed audit there would conflate "produced" with "accepted". Leaving it uncommitted is refused: the auditor container is ephemeral and the evidence would be destroyed.
authorization: The V4 audit prompt forbids the auditor from committing or pushing. The Head Researcher, as the owner of that prompt and of this decision, narrowly releases that constraint for one purpose only: pushing the audit namespace to the named audit/... branch. The auditor must not modify any tracked file, must not commit to main, must not merge, and must not open a pull request. The constraint exists to stop an auditor mutating sealed artifacts or self-accepting into canonical state, not to destroy evidence; a fresh audit/... branch touching no tracked file serves that purpose intact.
hashes_file_ruling: INDEPENDENT_V4_EXECUTION_AUDIT_HASHES.json must NOT be rewritten to embed its own commit SHA. A file cannot hash the commit that contains it, and a second commit would break its self-verification. The commit SHA is recorded in this ledger instead, exactly as L-003 recorded the V3 audit commit.
verification: In the Head Researcher container the namespace audit_v52_t4f1_execution_candidate_v4_independent_audit_2026_09_01/ is ABSENT and no V4 audit branch exists on the remote; the package is unreachable and unverified here. These agree with the L-006 preparation measurements, but agreement of relayed numbers is not acceptance.
outcome_boundary: Unchanged. Task 4F1 preregistration BLOCKED, run BLOCKED, retrieval-quality outcome access FORBIDDEN. No --mode run, no --mode finalize, no HMAC key, no valid authorization.
status: IN_PROGRESS
next_single_action: Auditor pushes the package to audit/v52-t4f1-v4-independent-2026-09-01; the Head Researcher then re-derives its hashes and boundary declarations independently before any acceptance or sealing entry is written.
role_separation_note: This V4 audit was performed by a different agent from the one that prepared V4, which closes the dual-role weakness disclosed in L-003. That strengthens the audit's standing once its artifacts are actually verifiable.
environment_warning: Tag pushes are refused in this project environment with HTTP 403 for annotated and lightweight tags alike, while branch pushes succeed. If the auditor's push of a tag fails, that is environmental and not a repository-integrity event; the branch is the anchor.

### L-009

```text
timestamp_utc: 2026-09-02T15:00:00Z
actor_role: Continuity Lead (sole writer) recording the co-chair's REQUEST CHANGES decision
predecessor_commit_or_tag: L-008 / a5ee73fa2df29683a8a68fcb2f17461a607cc011
scope: Record the co-chair review of L-008, independently confirm its blocking finding, withdraw the V4 package seal, and set the V5 remediation route. No candidate byte is modified by this entry.
changed_or_created_paths: ops/CURRENT_STATE.json; docs/CONTINUITY_LEDGER.md; START_HERE_V52_4F1.md; docs/RESEARCH_PROGRAM_STATUS_2026-08-31.md
cochair_decision: REQUEST CHANGES. The V4 independent-audit evidence is accepted as authentic, hash-consistent and valid for its nine implementation gates. The broader L-008 conclusion that the complete V4 execution package may stand sealed is NOT co-signed.
blocking_finding_CC01: task4f1_execution_candidate_v4_2026_09_01/EXECUTION_SPEC.md, a file bound in PAYLOAD_HASHES.json at SHA256 de1d3689f113607c1a7d5425c5795c03faeeeae74e7a4bb75953a57c9691f748, contains two mutually exclusive normative canary definitions. Line 44 still requires the raw array SHA256 values to reproduce the superseded V3 digests 25089a07... and e422490a..., while lines 110-112 require the V4 sign-code digests 7365b6c4... and 5e40a5d1.... Only the latter is implemented and audited. V3 was superseded precisely because those raw-float digests vary across conformant BLAS dispatches, so the line-44 requirement is unsatisfiable on conformant hardware and would mislead a cold-start successor.
independent_confirmation: The Continuity Lead reproduced CC-01 directly by grep on the bound file; both digest pairs are present at the stated lines and the file is listed in PAYLOAD_HASHES.json. The finding is CONFIRMED.
root_cause_and_accountability: This defect was introduced by the Continuity Lead when preparing V4. EXECUTION_SPEC.md was updated by a blanket "V3" -> "V4" label substitution plus an appended canary section, which bumped labels and added the correct specification without removing the superseded normative text. The V4 audit prompt scoped Gate 2 to runner source and AST change isolation and required no semantic contradiction scan across bound prose, so the audit could pass all nine gates while this defect survived. Both the authoring method and the prompt scope are at fault; neither is the auditor's error.
tier_table_verification: The co-chair's frozen tier composition table was independently recomputed from the sealed cohort and matches exactly: 100K 355 questions / 20 archives / 3.08 mean gold / 96 structural ALL@3 zeros; 500K 629 / 35 / 4.26 / 168; 1M 553 / 31 / 8.59 / 253; 10M 175 / 10 / 7.47 / 70; totals 1712 questions and 96 archives.
agreements_reached: (1) Single writer confirmed: the Continuity Lead alone writes CURRENT_STATE.json and the ledger; the co-chair writes only review artifacts on a separate branch and holds approval/veto. (2) Seal mechanism: detached attestation is the agreed correct mechanism; an audited candidate is never edited in place to change its historical submission status. V5 is required for the CONTENT defect, not to mutate the V4 seal. (3) Scientific route: option B. (4) Tier-stratified analysis is the primary scientific interest, bounded as an association across fixed benchmark strata and never as a causal context-length effect.
outcome_boundary: Unchanged. No --mode run, no --mode finalize, no HMAC key, no valid authorization, no real ranking, no retrieval-quality outcome computed, read or reported.
status: BLOCKED
next_single_action: Build the V5 candidate namespace correcting CC-01 and the stale V3 text, keeping the execution runner byte-identical to the accepted V4 runner, and write the V5 independent-audit prompt including a mandatory cross-payload semantic-consistency gate.
open_item_for_cochair: The co-chair review was relayed in chat and is NOT yet a pushed artifact, so it cannot be hash-bound. The same standard applied to the V4 auditor and to the Continuity Lead applies here: the review must be pushed to a co-chair branch so this ledger entry can cite its exact commit and file SHA256. Until then this entry cites the decision content only.

accountability: These are the Continuity Lead's defects. CC-01 was repaired, but V5's answer to CC-01 was a general mechanism, and the mechanism does not hold. Its root error is structural rather than clerical: the gate trusted a hand-written mirror list, so it could only ever verify what its author remembered to declare, and it compared raw text per line, so any reformatting defeated it. A gate that depends on its author's memory and formatting cannot support the claim "no superseded load-bearing literal survives anywhere".
correct_v6_direction: The burden must be inverted. The gate should DISCOVER every occurrence of every authoritative value across the bound closure and require each discovered occurrence to be declared, failing on any undeclared one, instead of checking a list the author wrote. Text must be normalised before matching, at minimum case-folded with whitespace and line breaks collapsed, so wrapping or casing cannot hide a literal. Missing concepts must be added, the attestation path must be a real resolvable path with an actual V5 record, and the duplicate status field must be removed.
outcome_boundary: Unchanged. Task 4F1 preregistration BLOCKED, run BLOCKED, retrieval-quality outcome access FORBIDDEN. Nothing is sealed.
status: BLOCKED
next_single_action: Decide, with the co-chair when available, whether to prepare V6 on the inverted-burden design. Preparing V6 exceeds the standing approval 4bd32782, which covered V5 preparation and its audit only.

### L-015

```text
timestamp_utc: 2026-09-03T11:00:00Z
actor_role: Continuity Lead (sole writer)
predecessor_commit_or_tag: L-014 / 294ec13
scope: Claim the V6 remediation stage answering the BLOCKED V5 delta audit. V1-V5 candidates, preflight packages, seals, manifests, audits and acceptance records remain byte-for-byte untouched; V6 is a new namespace.

V6 is prepared under Continuity Lead authority alone and is explicitly marked as requiring co-chair ratification before any sealing decision. Preparing it commits nothing irreversible: nothing is sealed, and V6 must itself pass an independent audit.
design_change: V5 failed because its gate trusted a hand-written mirror list and compared raw text per line, so it could only verify what its author declared and any reformatting defeated it. V6 inverts the burden. The gate sweeps every bound payload for value-shaped tokens (64-hex, 40-hex and V52_ identifiers), and every token it discovers must be attributed to a declared concept AND found only at that concept's authoritative path or a declared mirror. An unattributed token fails as a missing concept; an attributed token in an undeclared location fails as an unlabelled second normative source. Matching runs on normalised text with whitespace removed and case folded, so line wrapping and uppercase hex cannot hide a literal.
also_fixed: The duplicate status field in PAYLOAD_HASHES.json is removed; the attestation authoritative path becomes a real resolvable file with an actual V6 record; the five runner-enforced constants and tie priority gain declared concepts.
outcome_boundary: Unchanged. No --mode run, no --mode finalize, no HMAC key, no valid authorization, no real ranking, no retrieval-quality outcome computed, read or reported.
status: IN_PROGRESS
next_single_action: Build V6, prove the gate blocks the exact two variants that defeated V5, then commission an independent audit.
```

### L-018

```text
timestamp_utc: 2026-09-03T18:00:00Z
actor_role: Continuity Lead (sole writer)
predecessor_commit_or_tag: L-017 / 5c9416a
scope: Accept the commissioned V6 independent delta audit and record its BLOCKED verdict against the Continuity Lead's own inverted-burden gate. No candidate, seal, manifest, audit or corpus byte is touched.
changed_or_created_paths: ops/CURRENT_STATE.json; docs/CONTINUITY_LEDGER.md; START_HERE_V52_4F1.md; docs/RESEARCH_PROGRAM_STATUS_2026-08-31.md
audit_anchor: branch audit/v52-t4f1-v6-independent-2026-09-03 at commit c88455bad145e0e8b26e26545d1050e1ac67ba52, hashes file at SHA256 b26c91d802fde17e9c5bcaa66899bdecf7a094659668926f64900287bb803175.
verdict: BLOCKED - DO NOT SEAL / DO NOT PREREGISTER / DO NOT RUN TASK 4F1.
package_verification: The hashes file self-verifies 36/36 with 0 mismatch, 0 missing and 0 undeclared. The auditor modified no tracked file: its three commits add 37 files, all inside its own audit namespace, with no bytecode, corpus or virtualenv staged, and the V6 candidate bytes are untouched. Boundary is clean: 0 run, 0 finalize, 0 preflight CLI invocations, 0 HMAC environment sets, 3 forbidden forms refused before launch, no sealed namespace or corpus modified.
findings_independently_reproduced: 31 evasions were reported. The Continuity Lead re-tested nine against its own package and ALL NINE escaped: a novel digest hidden by a hex-adjacent word, the live cohort digest relocated to README.md the same way, a deprecated literal split by a zero-width space, a deprecated literal in a non-UTF-8 bound payload, a deprecated literal in the detached attestation which lies outside the swept closure, a novel digest under the key supersedes_candidate_addendum which the prefix rule silently accepted, a blanket exempt class appended to the map, the deprecated flag flipped to false, and the estimand denominator moved from 1712 to 1713. The audit is correct.
root_cause: The defects are structural, not a list of bugs to patch. Three of them are fatal to the approach itself. First, the gate's configuration lives inside the package it audits, so the subject can disarm its own auditor - appending an exempt class or clearing a deprecated flag defeats it entirely. Second, scanning arbitrary prose in arbitrary encodings for arbitrary re-encodings of a value is an unwinnable arms race: zero-width characters, markup interleaving, base64, decimal, JSON key splitting and non-UTF-8 payloads each defeat it, and closing one form does not close the class. Third, the estimand-critical values are short - 1712, 96, 392, the seeds, thresholds and tolerances - so they are not token-shaped and lie outside any such sweep by construction. The V6 ledger disclosed that third limit; the audit showed it is not theoretical but directly exploitable to change the denominator.
approach_conclusion: Two general contradiction-detectors have now been built and both were comprehensively defeated. A third would fail for the same reasons. The proportionate remedy for CC-01, which was a contradiction in prose, is to REMOVE the normative prose rather than to police it: bound documents should reference the runner's constants by name and never restate a digest, seed, count or threshold, so that each value appears exactly once by construction and there is nothing left to contradict. Derived text should be generated from the single source at package time rather than hand-written and then checked. Any verifier that remains must live outside the candidate, pinned on main, so the package cannot disarm it.
proportionality_note_for_the_chairs: The retrieval runner has been byte-identical and audit-confirmed since V4 - B1, B2 and B3 were established repaired by the V4 audit and nothing since has touched execution behaviour. V5 and V6 were both about documentation and metadata consistency inside the candidate package. That is a legitimate integrity concern, but the chairs should decide explicitly whether continued escalation of the checker is still proportionate to the risk it addresses, or whether shrinking the bound surface closes the issue at a fraction of the cost.
outcome_boundary: Unchanged. Task 4F1 preregistration BLOCKED, run BLOCKED, retrieval-quality outcome access FORBIDDEN. Nothing is sealed.
status: BLOCKED
next_single_action: The Head Researcher and co-chair decide the approach rather than approving another patch. The Continuity Lead does not self-seal and will not build a third scanner without that decision.
```

proportionality: A single ledger entry rather than a claim/close pair. This stage writes a draft that seals nothing and authorizes nothing, so the full stage ceremony would have been overhead rather than protection. That judgement is recorded so a successor sees it was deliberate.
outcome_free_confirmation: The draft contains no BEAM retrieval-quality result. Every figure is either a structural fact recomputed from the sealed cohort or a previously accepted LongMemEval/LoCoMo number. Scanned for outcome leakage before commit.
substantive_findings: Two composition facts were measured rather than assumed. First, ability composition is balanced within every tier (100K 36-40, 500K 69-70, 1M 58-62, 10M 16-20), so ability is NOT a cross-tier confounder; this resolves a concern the co-chair raised. Second, gold cardinality is NOT balanced, and because Fractional Recall@3 is capped at min(1, 3/|gold|) the mean achievable ceiling differs by tier: 0.867 at 100K, 0.836 at 500K, 0.721 at 1M, 0.776 at 10M. That ceiling profile is NON-MONOTONIC in tier, which is analytically useful and is fixed now: an observed monotone trend across tiers cannot be explained by the ceiling, whereas a non-monotone one plausibly can.
design_decisions_prespecified: Primary family is four tier-specific question-weighted contrasts of Native minus the five-seed Haar mean on Fractional Recall@3, sign convention positive means Native better, mapping the accepted LongMemEval and LoCoMo results to +15.926 pp and +9.884 pp. A ceiling-normalised companion is mandatory because the achievable range differs by tier. ALL@3 is reported but excluded from cross-tier scale claims because gold cardinality drives it. The signed-permutation control is invalidating rather than supportive. No population inference, no p-values and no multiplicity correction, because the cohort is a fixed benchmark and no null-hypothesis test is performed; the absence is stated so it is not mistaken for an omission. The twenty nuisance trials are integrity checks, never a variance source. Any ordered pattern across tiers is an association across fixed strata and must never be reported as a causal effect of context length or as a degradation law.
honest_prior_recorded: The draft states before any outcome is visible that if all four tiers return the same direction and similar magnitude, the marginal value over the two existing benchmarks is modest and must be reported as modest. This is written down so a flat result cannot later be inflated, and so the informative outcomes (a changing profile, or a failure at some tier) are named in advance.
outcome_boundary: Unchanged. Task 4F1 preregistration BLOCKED, run BLOCKED, retrieval-quality outcome access FORBIDDEN.
status: PASS
next_single_action: Head Researcher and co-chair review the draft. The packaging question is separate and should be closed by removing restated values from bound documents rather than by a third automated scanner.
```

### L-020

```text
timestamp_utc: 2026-09-03T21:00:00Z
actor_role: Continuity Lead (sole writer), acting on a Head Researcher REQUEST CHANGES
predecessor_commit_or_tag: L-019 / fb27767
scope: Apply the six Head Researcher change requests to the preregistration draft and to the continuity documentation. No candidate, seal, manifest, audit or corpus byte is touched. Nothing is preregistered or sealed.
changed_or_created_paths: docs/v52/task4f1/TASK4F1_PREREGISTRATION_DRAFT_2026-09-03.md; START_HERE_V52_4F1.md; docs/RESEARCH_PROGRAM_STATUS_2026-08-31.md; ops/CURRENT_STATE.json; docs/CONTINUITY_LEDGER.md
item_1_outcome_partition: Section 6 was genuinely overlapping - "partial replication" and "disconfirmation" could both fire on the same result. Replaced with a two-step scheme: integrity is resolved first, and if it holds, exactly one of full replication (all four D_t > 0), heterogeneous replication (at least one above and at least one at or below zero) or no replication (all four at or below zero) applies. These three are exhaustive and non-overlapping by construction. A negative tier may additionally carry the descriptor "tier-local direction reversal", which attaches to the tier and never overrides the single global category.
item_2_ceiling_argument: The draft had claimed a monotone observed profile "cannot be explained" by the non-monotone ceiling profile. That was an overclaim and is withdrawn. The corrected statement is that the ceiling profile does not MECHANICALLY IMPOSE a monotone ordering; the ceiling may still contribute through interaction with archive length, gold structure or unmeasured composition, and nothing measured here excludes that. Raw D_t and ceiling-normalised D_t^norm must now be interpreted jointly, and a conclusion supported by only one of them is not reported as supported.
item_3_ability_claim: "Ability is NOT a cross-tier confounder" was too strong: marginal balance says nothing about the joint distribution, since ability could covary with gold cardinality or archive length inside a tier. Narrowed to the established claim that no obvious marginal ability-composition imbalance explains tier differences, with ability-stratified reporting explicitly retained as mandatory and explicitly NOT discharged by the measurement.
item_4_haar_target: The comparator is now defined explicitly as a finite mean over exactly the five preregistered seeds 43001-43005, treated as a fixed enumerated comparator. No inference is made to the full Haar-rotation distribution and no claim about "Haar rotations in general" follows. The five per-seed aggregates and their min-max spread are sensitivity diagnostics on that fixed comparator, not a sampling distribution and not a basis for interval estimation. The same restriction is stated for the five ITQ seeds.
item_5_entry_points: START_HERE and RESEARCH_PROGRAM_STATUS still described V6 as prepared and audit-pending while CURRENT_STATE correctly recorded it BLOCKED. Both are realigned: V6 BLOCKED with 31 evasions, the scanner strategy abandoned by Head Researcher decision, the runner byte-identical and audit-confirmed since V4, and the current work identified as the preregistration draft under review. Audit prompts under prompts/ are now labelled as historical records of completed audits rather than pending work.
item_6_governance: The stale note that the co-chair was unavailable is removed, since a Head Researcher decision arrived on 2026-09-03. The record now separates what is verifiable from what is not: the Head Researcher holds final approval and veto and the Continuity Lead never self-seals; the 2026-09-02 co-chair artifacts remain valid and hash-bound at 776c45f3 and 4bd32782; but the 2026-09-03 decision was relayed as chat text rather than a pushed artifact, so it is recorded by content and is NOT hash-verifiable here. Which agent currently occupies the co-chair seat is not established by any artifact this session can verify, so no current occupant is asserted rather than guessed.
live_unratified_item: One item survives as genuinely open - the authorization template declares V52_T4F1_RUN_AUTHORIZATION_V4 rather than a "V5"-labelled schema, because the byte-identical runner verifies that literal. It must be ratified before any sealing. The other two formerly unratified items are moot because V5 and the V6 prompt are both closed.
outcome_boundary: Unchanged. Task 4F1 preregistration BLOCKED, run BLOCKED, retrieval-quality outcome access FORBIDDEN. The draft remains outcome-free; it was scanned for BEAM outcome leakage before commit.
status: PASS
next_single_action: Head Researcher approves or rejects the revised draft on its exact bytes. Packaging is separately closed by removing duplicated normative values from bound documents; no third scanner is authorized or will be built.
```

Its merge base with main is d95ab8ec, the sealed state it reviewed; it adds exactly two files and modifies nothing else.
defect_is_mine_and_was_reproduced_not_accepted: The finding is against this session's own work and was reproduced from bytes before being accepted. Recomputed directly from the sealed cohort CSV over its 1,712 eligible rows, the four tiers are 100K n=355 archives=20 zero-compression=259; 500K n=629 archives=35 zero-compression=461; 1M n=553 archives=31 zero-compression=300; 10M n=175 archives=10 zero-compression=105. Seal V1 binding 2 records 100K:355, 1M:629, 5M:553, 10M:175 and the same shift in ceiling_free_stratum_denominators and archive_counts. The 500K tier is therefore missing from the seal, a tier 5M that does not exist anywhere in the cohort or the draft was introduced, and the 500K and 1M labels are shifted onto the wrong counts. The approved draft's own table at lines 50 to 53 names the tiers 100K, 500K, 1M, 10M. D1 is confirmed exactly as stated.
second_defect_confirmed: D2 and D3 are also correct and are the more serious finding. tools/verify_preregistration_seal.py checked binding 2 only by the cohort file digest and never recomputed the tier dictionaries written beside it; it checked binding 7 only by asserting the list had four entries and never compared those entries to section 8 of the approved draft; it checked binding 8 only for a non-empty string. Its PASS therefore did not establish what L-032 claimed for it. The claim "every binding was re-derived from bytes" was too strong and is retracted here in the same words it was made.
why_this_matters_more_than_the_typo: A wrong tier label inside a normative binding is a mislabelled freeze - a later reader binding analysis to that seal would attribute 629 questions to 1M and 553 to a tier that does not exist. But the wrong label is recoverable in one commit; a verifier that reports PASS without testing what it claims to test is what allowed the wrong label to be published under a PASS, and would have allowed the next one too. The repair therefore targets the verifier first and the metadata second.
excluded_archives_reconfirmed: Recomputed over tier::conversation_id identity, the cohort holds 100 distinct archives of which 96 are eligible; the four excluded are exactly 1M::5, 1M::26, 1M::33 and 1M::34, matching the standing brief and the approved draft.

no_conclusions_supplied: The auditor is given no verdict, gate result, rho value or finding from this session. verdict: AUDIT PASS WITH CAVEATS. G1, G2, G3, G5, G9, G10 PASS as established. G4 PASS by end-to-end re-execution on BOTH benchmarks. G8 PASS on semantics. G7 PASS via a control the auditor supplied itself. G6 splits: PASS on the verdict, FAIL on the reported precision. G11 completed: the case against weakens but does not defeat the result.
the_decisive_new_fact: The auditor did not merely check the reported work; it ran the missing control. Its RANDPART_32_64_HAAR arm applies a Haar rotation within a RANDOM 32-subset of the 96 coordinates and within its complement, matched in block sizes to the preregistered intervention. On LoCoMo it yields rho_2 = 0.9488647823652118 against the Head32/Tail64 arm's 0.10744461886682183. The Continuity Lead recomputed both from the arm seed values: exact match, and every partition verified to be exactly 32 distinct indices inside 0..95. Delta = 0.8414. This session's 2026-09-04 status report had proposed exactly this control with a frozen falsification threshold of Delta <= 0.05 killing the lead and Delta >= 0.25 strengthening it. The observed Delta is 0.841. The confound that any 32/64 block structure would suffice is REJECTED on LoCoMo: it is the identity of the coordinates, not the block sizes, that carries the effect. The control was NOT run on LongMemEval, so position-dependence there remains unestablished.
reproduction_strength: The auditor re-ran the committed LoCoMo runner end-to-end on Python 3.11.15 / numpy 2.3.5 against CI's Python 3.13, and reproduced locomo_head_tail_seed_results.csv, subset_r3.csv, pairwise.csv and hard_negative_rescue.csv BIT-IDENTICALLY by SHA-256. Only the summary JSON and the checkpoint markdown differ, which is environment metadata rather than result content. Cross-interpreter bit-identity on the result CSVs is a stronger reproduction than this program has obtained anywhere else, including on the sealed track.
caveats_accepted_as_binding: Four narrowings are required by the audit and are recorded here as owed by the research track, not by this session. One, stop presenting rho_2 at 9 to 17 significant figures; report 0.107 +/- 0.047 and 0.157 +/- 0.025 over five draws and state that the denominator's own sampling error is excluded. Two, disclose that LoCoMo seed 56001 inverts the sign of L_2 - per-seed rho_2 of -0.0672 - which is the single most informative fact about the stability of the LoCoMo magnitude and appears nowhere in the checkpoint. Three, qualify "cross-benchmark" as two frozen datasets under one shared pipeline, a dataset-level replication and not an independent one. Four, replace the SHARDED_EXACT label with a claim that is true as stated: shard-invariant semantics are demonstrable and the aggregate reproduces to about 1e-16, but bit-level exactness is asserted rather than shown.
independent_confirmation_by_this_session: The Continuity Lead did not accept the gate table. It recomputed both arms of the G7 control from their seed values, checked every random partition for size and range, verified the report digest against its sidecar, verified the merge base, and confirmed by targeted diff that nothing outside the audit namespace changed. The G2 divergence table was read and its three items are maintenance observations, not decision-changing deviations.
what_this_session_had_flagged_and_how_it_resolved: The status report's caveat C1 - five draws give a seed dispersion comparable to the effect - is UPHELD by the audit and converted into a required narrowing. The caveat C2 - no matched-size random-partition control - is RESOLVED IN FAVOUR of the spectral hypothesis on LoCoMo and remains open on LongMemEval. The caveat C6 - what "cross-benchmark" is entitled to mean - is UPHELD.
gate_status: The Head Researcher conditioned the boundary-localization preregistration on a passing audit. The audit passes with caveats, so the gate is OPEN. It is not automatic: no boundary-localization experiment is preregistered or run by this entry, which remains a separate Head Researcher decision, and the four narrowings are owed by the research track.
outcome_boundary: Unchanged. Task 4F1 scientific preregistration SEALED at Seal V3; Task 4F1 run BLOCKED; production authorization NONE; retrieval-quality outcome access FORBIDDEN. status: PASS
next_single_action: Report the verified audit to the Head Researcher for the boundary-localization decision. The four required narrowings are owed by the research track before the localization checkpoint cites these numbers. ### L-041

```text
timestamp_utc: 2026-09-04T21:30:00Z
actor_role: Continuity Lead (sole writer)
predecessor_commit_or_tag: L-040 / 33f58dd
scope: Write and record the systematic literature-scan prompt for the SIGN96 mechanism result. No experiment is proposed for execution, no research artifact is touched, no seal or candidate byte changes.
changed_or_created_paths: prompts/V52_SIGN_MECHANISM_LITERATURE_SCAN_PROMPT_2026-09-04.md; ops/CURRENT_STATE.json; docs/CONTINUITY_LEDGER.md
prompt_sha256: 9eaefd512428c05250a7747c95ba65a81c26d6252ab76a4080908e1eb1c092ee
why_written: Two exploratory scans were run in chat and neither was persisted. Their findings materially changed how the Head/Tail result should be worded, so leaving them in chat was a continuity defect: a later session reading GitHub alone would not know that the headline "rotation hurts" claim is already published theory. This prompt turns the scan into a commissioned, reproducible task with a pushed artifact.
findings_recorded_as_claims_not_facts: The prior scans surfaced nine claims, C1 to C9, and the prompt hands them to the scanner as hypotheses to confirm or refute rather than as results. The two load-bearing negatives are called out as such: that Xiao 2026 arXiv 2605.17524 considers only full Haar rotation and never partial, block-diagonal or selective rotation (C2), and that QuIVer arXiv 2605.02171 uses no rotation at all and performs no principal-component or variance-band analysis (C3). The core novelty claim, C9, is that no published work uses subspace-restricted rotation as a causal ablation to localize where a retrieval representation's advantage lives. The prompt states that refuting C2, C3 or C9 is the most valuable outcome available, because it would shrink what this program may claim.
version_discrepancy_flagged_for_resolution: Two different contents were served for arXiv 2605.17524 - a PDF whose Theorem 2 reads "Rotation uniformizes coordinate variances" and an HTML v1 with a different title whose Theorem 2 reads "Weak correlations accumulate", with different corollary numbering. The prompt requires one version to be pinned before anything from that paper may be quoted in a project artifact. Nothing from it is quoted in any committed artifact yet.
calibration_against_a_wrong_reading: The prompt states explicitly that the leading block alone is weak - Head32-only R@3 about 0.083 against Tail64-only about 0.215 on LoCoMo - so the scanner does not search on the assumption that the head carries the information. That reading was implied in an earlier chat summary by this session and is not supported by the project's own diagnostics.
method_discipline_required: A search-engine summary is declared not to be evidence, and an abstract is declared insufficient for any claim about what a paper does NOT do. The scanner must record identifier, version, date, venue and the depth actually read for every source, must log every query so a negative can be attacked, must distinguish established from not falsified, and is warned that a summarizer asked a leading question tends to answer yes - a failure this session experienced directly.
prior_art_distinction_mandated: The report must separate prior art for the CONSTRUCTION - building codes block-wise, as R2PCAH and OPQ eigenvalue allocation do - from prior art for the ABLATION USE, which is what this program claims. Conflating them would either overstate or understate the position.
prohibitions_carried: No Task 4F1 run, finalize, authorization, HMAC key or outcome access. No modification of any sealed artifact, candidate, manifest, corpus, audit namespace, or of the mechanism research branch. No experiments executed. No paywall circumvention - access must be requested legitimately or the failure recorded.
outcome_boundary: Unchanged. Task 4F1 scientific preregistration SEALED at Seal V3; run BLOCKED; production authorization NONE; retrieval-quality outcome access FORBIDDEN. The Head/Tail audit stands at AUDIT PASS WITH CAVEATS and the boundary-localization gate remains open but undecided. The V7 execution package remains BLOCKED at 16dc613.
status: PASS
next_single_action: Deliver the literature-scan prompt to whoever will run it, and await its pushed artifact for independent hash verification. The scan does not gate the boundary-localization decision, which remains a separate Head Researcher call.
```

### L-043

```text
timestamp_utc: 2026-09-05T07:10:00Z
actor_role: Continuity Lead / co-chair (sole writer on main)
predecessor_commit_or_tag: L-042 / 45e6dde
scope: Independently verify and record the boundary-localization independent audit. Nothing is modified on the research branch, the audit branch, or any frozen artifact. Main carries this record only.
changed_or_created_paths: ops/CURRENT_STATE.json; docs/CONTINUITY_LEDGER.md
audit_verified: Branch audit/v52-boundary-localization-independent-2026-09-04, commit 27f50f44490b419921b1d634f11006529b290a6f, report audit_v52_boundary_localization_independent_2026_09_04/AUDIT_REPORT.md at SHA256 80f6a3aa0da5e9e81442c486cb35f11c3bf91a4384840f8cb53ff2d2d90965f3, recomputed from the fetched blob and matched against its committed .sha256 sidecar. Its merge base with the research branch is the audited commit 1a33ef0 exactly. Diffed against that base it adds 21 files, ALL of them under its own audit namespace; a targeted diff for anything outside that namespace returns empty. The auditor audited and did not edit.
verdict: AUDIT PASS WITH CAVEATS. Nineteen gates. A through R all PASS and all as ESTABLISHED - positively demonstrated from bytes or computation, none resting on not-falsified. S completed with the adjudication that the case against WEAKENS the result without defeating it. No gate FAILED and no gate is NOT ESTABLISHED.
integrity_findings: The auditor reports no defect of integrity, no undisclosed run, no post-hoc tuning, no threshold or seed alteration, and no Task 4F1 contact. Gate D is unusually strong: the GitHub Actions API shows total_count 1, run_number 1, run_attempt 1 on each workflow, event push, head_sha equal to the trigger commit, and neither workflow declares workflow_dispatch, repository_dispatch or schedule, so no manual re-trigger path exists at all. Gate A establishes precedence structurally through ancestry assertions rather than through author-controlled timestamps, and notes that the seal's declared package_head_before_seal equals the actual first parent of the seal commit, a binding not under narrative control.
gate_k_is_the_one_that_matters: The auditor rebuilt all 120 seed-level R@3 and rho values from the per-question CSVs ALONE, deliberately excluding the summary JSONs and per-seed CSVs as inputs and using them only as comparison targets. Worst absolute deviation 2.04e-15. This is the reconstruction the previous stage could not support, and it is why persisting per-question records was worth doing.
caveat_independently_reproduced_by_this_session: The Continuity Lead did not accept the Gate S finding. Working from the per-question gzip directly, it rebuilt native from per-question records at 0.23654714666441043 against the frozen 0.23654714666441054, a difference of 1.1e-16, and reproduced rho values 0.0824 for B32 and 0.3034 for B48 on LoCoMo. It then ran its own paired question-level bootstrap on the load-bearing exclusion, LoCoMo B48, with 2000 resamples: P(rho <= 0.25) = 0.166, bootstrap sd 0.0551, 95 percent interval 0.194 to 0.407. The auditor reported approximately 0.21 and a bootstrap SE of 0.063 to 0.088. The two estimates differ in resampling scheme but agree in magnitude and in conclusion, and this session's figure is slightly MORE pessimistic. The finding stands independently.
the_binding_caveat: The reported standard errors are seed-panel only, conditional on the frozen denominator. Adding the question-sampling component, which the per-question persistence finally makes computable, gives an uncertainty three to five times larger. The position-dependence half of the claim survives overwhelmingly - the auditor reports RANDOM32 at minus 50 and minus 32 standard errors and a bootstrap probability of sufficiency of 0.0000 on both datasets. The uniqueness half does not survive at the confidence its presentation implies: S_common = {32} holds in only about 73 percent of resamples, with {32,48} at about 17 percent and the empty set at about 10 percent, and it rests entirely on the single LoCoMo B48 exclusion at rho 0.303 whose reversal probability is about 0.17 to 0.21.
narrowed_claim_accepted: Both mandatory labels remain correct and must still be reported together, but the LOCALIZATION half must be reported as a lead conditional on the ten-seed panel and the inherited denominator, not as an established property of the tested grid. The POSITION-DEPENDENCE half needs no such hedge: matched random partitions using the identical numeric Q32/Q64 destroy almost all of the advantage, Delta32 about 0.85 on LoCoMo and about 0.83 on LongMemEval. Among the five boundaries, 32/64 is sufficient on both datasets and 16/80, 24/72 and 64/32 are sufficient on neither; LongMemEval also admits 48/48 outright.
what_is_now_established_versus_conditional: ESTABLISHED - the effect depends on WHICH coordinates form the leading block and not merely on the presence of a 32/64 block structure; this is now demonstrated on both datasets with fresh seeds, and on LoCoMo it was also demonstrated by a separate auditor-supplied control in the previous stage. CONDITIONAL - that 32 is the unique sufficient boundary within the tested grid; that rests on one fragile exclusion and is roughly a 73 percent event.
carried_forward_as_owed_by_the_research_track: Per the governance rule that binding caveats are recorded before any next preregistration and the frozen original result is never rewritten, the research track owes an ADDITIVE post-audit interpretation addendum on its own branch, mirroring what it did after the Head/Tail audit. This session does not author it, because the research artifacts are not this session's to write and the frozen checkpoint must not be edited. Until that addendum lands, no next preregistration in this line should cite the localization half without the conditional wording above.
still_open_and_not_settled_by_this_audit: The earlier matched random-partition null control at 410bcf5 still carries an audit dispatch record with NO auditor branch; its LongMemEval position result has never been independently audited, although the localization stage now reproduces matched random partitions on both datasets with fresh seeds. The Full-Haar denominator remains inherited with its own sampling uncertainty unquantified anywhere in the package - the auditor names this in Gate S and it is now the single largest un-costed source of uncertainty in the line.
outcome_boundary: Unchanged. Task 4F1 scientific preregistration SEALED at Seal V3; run BLOCKED; production authorization NONE; retrieval-quality outcome access FORBIDDEN. Gate R establishes zero Task 4F1 contact by the stage and by the auditor, and this session accessed no Task 4F1 outcome. The audit branch touches no Task 4F1 path.
stop_rule_status: The stop rule that held all further mechanism work has now been discharged by a passing audit. That self-classification is accepted and is the correct one.
independently_reproduced_from_this_side: The review's direct contrast figures were recomputed here from the per-seed data and match exactly - LoCoMo B32 minus RANDOM32 = 8.448609 percentage points, LongMemEval = 13.203121 percentage points. The reviewer is right that this is an algebraic restatement of the published arms rather than a new preregistered endpoint, and that its uncertainty is not estimated.
correction_1_accepted_against_this_sessions_own_wording: L-043 wrote that S_common = {32} is "about a 73 percent event, not a settled fact". That wording is wrong and is corrected here. S_common = {32} is a DETERMINISTIC DESCRIPTIVE FACT: it is exactly what the frozen decision rule returns on the persisted panel, and question resampling does not make a computed fact 73 percent true. The 72.95 percent figure is a FREQUENCY under one empirical bootstrap, conditional on the seed panel, the fixed denominator and that bootstrap's resampling assumptions. It is not a posterior probability of a hypothesis and not a guaranteed replication rate. The correct statement is: the frozen-panel result is exact, and its STABILITY UNDER QUESTION RESAMPLING is limited, resting on one exclusion near the threshold.
correction_2_accepted_against_this_sessions_own_computation: The reviewer's methodological objection applies to this session's own bootstrap as much as to the auditor's. The 0.166 figure in L-043 resampled questions independently and identically distributed, with NO clustering by conversation or archive, NO resampling of rotation seeds, and NO propagation of the inherited Full-Haar denominator's uncertainty. LoCoMo questions are nested within conversations and archives and are therefore not independent, so that interval is very likely too NARROW. Neither 0.166 nor the auditor's 0.2103 over 20000 resamples is a certified sampling interval; both are sensitivity analyses that legitimately challenge strong uniqueness language and legitimately certify nothing. The reviewer explicitly did not reproduce the 0.166 figure and does not accept it as numerically equivalent to the auditor's; that is correct and no equivalence was or is claimed.
correction_3_accepted: Absolute integrity phrasing must be bounded. Git ancestry and the Actions history establish ordering and observed execution provenance within the inspected records; they cannot establish that no private or local computation ever existed. Wherever L-042 or L-043 carried the auditor's phrase "no result could have existed before the preregistration bytes did", read instead "no undisclosed execution was found in the inspected records". The same bounding applies to words like unassailable: the position-dependence finding is strong within the measured contrast and the inspected design, and that is the claim.
correction_4_accepted: Block membership is the manipulated factor, not an identified mediator. Matching the numeric Q32 and Q64 while changing membership is strong design evidence, but the downstream changes to energy mixing, sign dependence and query relevance are not disentangled. The two datasets share one representation pipeline and are not independent methodological replications - already recorded, and reaffirmed.
correction_5_attribution_checked_before_accepting: The flagged Turkish phrase "32 tek basina yeterlidir" was located in the AUDITOR's own Turkish summary, section 9 of AUDIT_REPORT.md, not in this session's records, the checkpoint or the research artifacts. The objection is correct - B32 uses all 96 coordinates under a 32/64 partition and is not a Head32-only code - and it is correctly targeted at the audit report. This session's Turkish reporting said "the only boundary sufficient on both of the tested five", which does not carry that ambiguity, and the preferred formulation is adopted going forward: the 32/64 SPLIT satisfies the sufficiency rule on the frozen panel on both datasets.
literature_corrections_recorded: Two claims from this session's literature scan are corrected by the review. C8, that no published work applies binary or quantized retrieval representations to agent long-term-memory benchmarks, is REFUTED: Hippocampus, MLSys 2026, uses binary signatures for memory retrieval on both LoCoMo and LongMemEval. The earlier "empty intersection" statement was wrong and is withdrawn. C2, that Xiao arXiv 2605.17524 never examines anything but full rotation, is NARROWED rather than confirmed: version 2 carries shuffle and synthetic correlated-block probes that were not seen in the earlier reading, and its contrastive Gaussian assumptions in any case do not establish the mechanism for archive-local TF-IDF/SVD representations. C1 requires revision and C9 stands only as a search-qualified narrow claim. These belong in the literature-scan artifact when that scan is run; the prompt at prompts/V52_SIGN_MECHANISM_LITERATURE_SCAN_PROMPT_2026-09-04.md already frames C1 to C9 as claims to test, so no rewrite of it is needed - this entry records that two of them are already answered.
next_target_recorded_not_authorized: The reviewer proposes testing how much of the advantage comes from relative coordinate scale, using the identity that for any positive diagonal D, sign(xD) = sign(x), so a positive archive-derived rescaling preserves the native sign code exactly while changing sign(xDQ) after an orthogonal mixing Q. The decisive comparison is an INTERACTION - whether rescaling helps full mixing more than within-block mixing under paired queries and fixed rotations - not a reduction in the native-versus-full gap alone. Constraints the reviewer names and this entry carries forward: D must not be determined by query labels or outcomes; standard deviations must be defined on the exact centered archive representation rather than assumed equal to raw singular values; zero-variance coordinates and numerical underflow need fixed handling before any run; and norm and dot equality must be checked within each transformed representation and its rotation, not between the original and rescaled representations. This is a DIRECTION. It is not preregistered, not sealed, not authorized, and no seeds, workflow or trigger exist for it.
what_did_not_change: The audit verdict remains AUDIT PASS WITH CAVEATS. Gates A to R remain PASS. The frozen research bytes, the audit bytes and the checkpoint are untouched. Both mandatory labels still apply and still travel together. The research track still owes an ADDITIVE post-audit interpretation addendum on its own branch, now also incorporating the five wording corrections above, before any next preregistration cites the localization half.
stop_rule: The audit-wait stop was discharged by the passing audit in L-043 and is not re-imposed here. It is also not converted into permission: the next experiment remains a separate Head Researcher decision, and this review explicitly declines to release anything.
outcome_boundary: Unchanged. Task 4F1 scientific preregistration SEALED at Seal V3; run BLOCKED; production authorization NONE; retrieval-quality outcome access FORBIDDEN. The review branch touches no Task 4F1 path and this session accessed no Task 4F1 outcome.
status: PASS
next_single_action: Hand the five wording corrections and the two literature corrections to the research track so its additive post-audit addendum incorporates them, then await the Head Researcher decision on the coordinate-scale direction. Do not preregister or run it.

finding_1_identity_confirmed: sign(xD) = sign(x) held EXACTLY in float64 across every synthetic archive and query, and SCALED_NATIVE reproduced NATIVE identically, with zero degenerate coordinates at eps 1e-12. The draft's identity arm is implementable as specified rather than merely plausible.
finding_2_shape_reproduces: As CV(sigma) rises the synthetic model reproduces the qualitative shape observed on the real benchmarks - full-Haar mixing becomes catastrophic while block-32 mixing degrades far less. This shows only that a heterogeneity-driven account CAN produce this shape; it is not evidence about the real pipeline.
finding_3_the_draft_was_wrong_and_the_pilot_caught_it: The draft made the percentage-point interaction I = delta_full minus delta_block the primary quantity, with a supported band at I >= 3.0 pp. In the pilot I reaches 13 to 22 pp across the whole heterogeneous range and would have been read as strong support for scale participating differentially in full mixing. It is almost entirely a FLOOR ARTIFACT: frac_full and frac_block, the share of each arm's OWN loss that rescaling recovers, are both approximately 1.00, and on the fraction scale the interaction nearly vanishes - 0.023, 0.013, 0.009, 0.008, 0.007 as heterogeneity rises. Full mixing gains more percentage points only because it had more loss available. Had the draft been frozen as written it would have reported that rescaling helps full mixing much more than block mixing, when the correct reading in that model is that rescaling restores BOTH.
consequence_applied: The draft's section 7 is revised. The primary quantity is now frac_full and frac_block reported per arm, with proposed bands 0.70 and 0.20 and an explicit requirement that the bands admit frac greater than 1. The interaction survives only on the fraction scale and is demoted to secondary. The percentage-point quantities are retained as descriptive and are explicitly barred from carrying any verdict. This reintroduces a denominator, which the draft had tried to avoid; that is now a conscious trade, and the difference from the line's earlier trouble is that these denominators are measured INSIDE the experiment rather than inherited, and must be reported with their per-seed dispersion.
finding_4_overshoot: At low heterogeneity the rescaled rotated code BEAT the native code, frac 1.407 and 1.143. One is therefore not a ceiling, the bands must admit values above it, and no narrative may describe rescaling as merely recovering what was lost.
open_question_4_settled_empirically: The draft's fourth open design question - percentage points or a ratio - is now settled by measurement rather than preference, and settled AGAINST the draft's original stated preference. The remaining three open questions are untouched and still belong to the Head Researcher.

The generative model is a caricature with no inter-coordinate correlation structure, which the literature holds to carry 30 to 50 percent of ranking signal, so a real archive may differ in magnitude and possibly in kind.

### L-048

```text
timestamp_utc: 2026-09-05T11:15:00Z
actor_role: Continuity Lead / co-chair (sole writer on main)
predecessor_commit_or_tag: L-047 / 735543b

why_this_session_decided_rather_than_asked_again: The Head Researcher answered three successive requests for a decision with an instruction to continue. Two of the three remaining questions were empirically testable on synthetic data, so they were tested rather than re-asked; the third is a question about how the result can be attacked and was decided by argument. Each resolution is recorded with the evidence or the reasoning behind it, and one of the four was resolved AGAINST this document's own earlier stated preference.
q1_resolved_by_measurement: D = 1/sigma, not the gentler sigma to the minus one half. With 1/sigma the recovered fraction lands at approximately 1.00 at both heterogeneity levels tested, so the experiment can actually discriminate between scale explaining the damage and scale not explaining it. With sigma to the minus one half the fraction is 0.46 to 0.82 and drifts with heterogeneity, which would land inside a partial band by construction and answer nothing. The gentler rule is not the more cautious choice here; it is the less informative one.
q2_resolved_by_measurement: Per-archive sigma, not global. Per-archive matches the archive-local SVD the representation is built from and is tighter, with both arms near 1.00. A global sigma overshoots on the block arm at 1.096 and FLIPS THE SIGN of the fraction-scale interaction between settings, which is exactly the instability a mismatched scale introduces.
q3_resolved_by_argument: Fresh rotation seeds 59001 to 59010 rather than reusing the audited panel 58001 to 58010. Not decidable by measurement. Reuse would invite the objection that a panel already known to show the effect was chosen; fresh seeds cost nothing; and the boundary stage already showed that a fresh panel reproduces the shape, so tighter pairing with the audited stage buys little and costs the appearance of independence.
degenerate_stress_passed: Twelve of ninety-six coordinates were forced to approximately zero variance, giving 360 fallbacks across thirty archives. The epsilon 1e-12 fallback to d equal to one behaved correctly and the sign(xD) equals sign(x) identity survived exactly, with the recovered fraction still 0.97 to 0.99 under the chosen rule. This is the case most likely to appear in real archives and it does not break the design.
document_promoted: The status line now reads CANDIDATE PREREGISTRATION v3 - COMPLETE, AWAITING AUTHORIZATION. NOT SEALED, NOT TRIGGERED. Every design value is decided, so it is complete enough to be sealed as written, and the document says explicitly that freezing happens only on an explicit authorization and not by the document existing. what_remains_open_is_not_a_design_question: Where the real archives sit on the CV(sigma) axis. Writing code is preparation; the seal and the trigger are the authorization point. This entry stops exactly where that boundary is, and puts the code somewhere it cannot run even by accident.
non_triggerable_by_construction_not_by_promise: The runner is at drafts/v52/pending_runners/ rather than research/v52/, and the workflow at drafts/v52/pending_workflows/ rather than .github/workflows/. GitHub executes only workflows under the latter path, so while the file sits where it is it cannot fire by push, by schedule or by dispatch. This was verified after pushing: a search of the draft branch for an installed copy of the workflow returns zero. Installing the two files is part of what an authorization would authorize.
workflow_has_a_single_trigger_route_once_installed: It declares no workflow_dispatch, no repository_dispatch and no schedule, matching the boundary stage whose Actions history the auditor was able to certify as one run and one attempt. Its only route is a push creating a named trigger file that does not exist. It gates on a pre-run seal verification and on the self-test before it computes anything.
design_premises_enforced_as_aborts: Two things the design depends on are checked in code rather than assumed. SCALED_NATIVE must produce a distance matrix identical to NATIVE, and the sign identity is asserted with array equality rather than a tolerance; either failure raises and stops the run. Norm and dot invariance is asserted WITHIN each representation and its own rotation and never between the original and the rescaled representation, where it is not expected to hold - the specific error the peer reviewer warned about.
self_test_results: 23 checks on synthetic data, all passing. They cover the sign identity holding bit-identically; a deliberately NEGATIVE diagonal being REJECTED by the identity check; degenerate coordinates falling back to one with the identity surviving; rotation matrices orthogonal, block-diagonal at 32 and deterministic in their seed; invariance holding within a representation and demonstrably NOT claimed across the rescaling; frac behaving at zero, at one and above one and undefined on a zero denominator; and the bands admitting overshoot.
what_is_explicitly_not_validated: Anything requiring the corpus. The full pipeline has never been executed because the corpus is not in git, and the README says so rather than implying the code is proven.
two_practical_risks_stated_before_they_could_surface_mid_run: First, the invariance check compares a full Gram matrix and is quadratic in archive rows, where the audited stages compared query-to-archive dot products instead; if that dominates cost the correct fix is to narrow the check as those stages did and NOT to loosen the tolerance. Second, the synthetic dot error was 1.1e-13 against a 1e-12 tolerance - inside it, but with less headroom than the audited stages reported at about 1e-15, because the Gram matrix is larger; if a real run trips this it is information about the check's scale and not licence to raise TOL. Recording these now means neither can later be repaired quietly under time pressure.
scope_discipline: Only the LoCoMo runner is written. The LongMemEval sharded runner is deliberately not written yet, because the two practical risks above may change the invariance check, and writing the second runner before that is settled would mean fixing the same thing twice.
outcome_boundary: Unchanged. Task 4F1 scientific preregistration SEALED at Seal V3; run BLOCKED; production authorization NONE; retrieval-quality outcome access FORBIDDEN. No corpus was read, no benchmark retrieval was computed, and no Task 4F1 artifact or outcome was touched. The research branch is unmoved at 39bbfec and no frozen artifact was modified.
status: PASS
next_single_action: Head Researcher authorization remains the only gate. On authorization: settle the invariance-check scope, write the LongMemEval sharded runner, install runner and workflow, seal pre-run, trigger once.
```

the_recorded_claim_was_false_on_one_side: Every record since the boundary-localization audit has said the Full-Haar denominator's sampling uncertainty is quantified NOWHERE in the package, and named it the largest un-costed source of uncertainty in the line. For LongMemEval that has not been true since 2026-08-28. audit_v52_t4c3/AUDIT_REPORT.md, on canonical main, the ACCEPTED INDEPENDENT Task 4C3 audit, publishes the five per-seed full-Haar Fractional R@3 values for rotation seeds 43001 to 43005. Their mean agrees with the denominator in use to 6.7e-11, which is exactly the residual expected from that report's nine published decimals. The denominator in use IS that five-seed mean, and its dispersion follows directly.
what_the_dispersion_is: sample sd 0.014935802685146212; standard error 0.0066794940205021636 at n=5; 1.745 percent of the denominator; seed envelope 0.361395390 to 0.401278369; 95 percent Student-t interval 0.364174391 to 0.401258942.
breakdown_analysis_and_its_result: For each arm there is a single denominator value at which rho crosses the preregistered 0.25 gate. On LongMemEval every one of the six lies OUTSIDE the 95 percent interval; the tightest, B48, needs a 5.4 standard-error excursion. No LongMemEval verdict is threatened by the denominator's measured sampling uncertainty, so on that benchmark the item is discharged rather than carried forward.
locomo_is_not_closed_and_was_not_faked: No per-seed source for the LoCoMo denominator is committed anywhere, so its dispersion is not measurable and the LongMemEval standard error was deliberately NOT transferred to it. A control asserts the report carries no LoCoMo dispersion figure and no LoCoMo standard-error column. Only the consequence is bounded: LoCoMo B32 sufficiency needs a 48.12 percent denominator error to overturn, the most robust verdict in the line, while the B48 exclusion needs only 15.34 percent.
two_independent_lines_converge: The Gate S bootstrap flagged exactly one fragile item, the LoCoMo B48 exclusion at rho 0.303 on which the uniqueness of S_common={32} rests. This analysis uses no bootstrap and no resampling and reaches the same item, by a factor of five over the next arm. The established finding is untouched, resting on arms that need 54 to 197 percent denominator errors; what is named as conditional by two methods rather than one is the UNIQUENESS of 32 on the tested grid.
an_error_in_my_own_tooling_caught_by_my_own_control: The first version of the analysis stated the direction of the effect backwards, that rho falls as the denominator rises. The numerator is fixed, so rho RISES as the denominator rises toward native. The directional control failed and caught it before any number was published. It changed only direction labels; the breakdown points and magnitudes were unaffected. The control is retained with a comment recording why it exists, and the addendum discloses the error rather than quietly shipping the corrected file. 41/41 controls pass.
why_this_needed_no_gate: Head Researcher authorization gates the next experiment. It does not gate arithmetic on numbers already committed, nor reading an audit report that has been on main for nine days. Nothing here draws a seed, reads a corpus, or computes a retrieval.
outcome_boundary: Unchanged. Task 4F1 scientific preregistration SEALED at Seal V3; run BLOCKED; production authorization NONE; retrieval-quality outcome access FORBIDDEN. codex_finding_R1_reproduced_here_and_it_sharpens_my_own_caveat_rather_than_overturning_it: The review reports an aggregation-order ambiguity - A = the mean of per-seed gain/loss ratios, B = the ratio of seed-mean gain to seed-mean loss - and that the sealed runner's headline output is B. I recomputed both from the persisted summaries at 591e5d0f and Codex's figures reproduce exactly: LoCoMo block B 0.651643 vs A 0.445443, LoCoMo interaction B 0.074464 vs A 0.281744. Two things follow that the handover does not state. First, BOTH statistics are already recoverable from the published bytes, because the runner persists frac_*_per_seed alongside the seed-mean ratio, so R1's requirement to preserve both is satisfied by the existing record and needs no file change. Second, the divergence is almost entirely a BLOCK-arm phenomenon: on the full-mixing arm A and B agree to 0.0011 (LoCoMo) and 0.0006 (LongMemEval), while on the block arm they diverge by 0.206 (LoCoMo) and 0.008 (LongMemEval). The block denominator instability that the L-055 checkpoint already declared non-interpretable on LoCoMo is the same defect surfacing again through a second route. The headline full-mixing observation is not sensitive to the aggregation order; the comparative block/interaction claim is, and neither statistic rescues it.
codex_findings_R2_R5_accepted_as_recorded_not_adjudicated: R2 the originally missing uncertainty reports, since completed post-outcome; R3 the seal gate not covering the transitive LoCoMo dependency, retrospectively unchanged; R4 the result combiner accepting some malformed synthetic records, with the real 92,100 + 28,200 records separately verified PASS; R5 the preregistration's own section 7 and section 9 interpretation rules being inconsistent about what a positive mechanism reading requires. R5 is a defect in the frozen preregistration and must be recorded as an explicit deviation rather than closed by picking the favourable clause after the outcome. None of R1-R5 is adjudicated by this entry; adjudication belongs to the Head Researcher.
what_the_codex_evidence_establishes_and_what_it_does_not: ESTABLISHED - the sealed LoCoMo computation reproduces exactly from verified raw data in a single authorized invocation (92,100 rows, maximum numerical score difference 0.0, disclosed CRLF and gzip-timestamp byte differences, largest diagnostic difference 1.42e-14 under a 1e-12 tolerance). NOT ESTABLISHED - the comparative mechanism claim: all interaction percentile intervals under both aggregation definitions and all three resampling schemes span zero, and every full-arm interval crosses the 0.70 band boundary, so the LoCoMo MOST versus LongMemEval PARTIAL distinction cannot be carried as a population-level category difference. Explicitly NOT a significance test and NOT evidence that no mechanism exists.
consequence_for_this_sessions_own_records: the L-055 checkpoint's headline labels survive but must be narrowed. [COORDINATE-SCALE PARTICIPATION LEAD - MOST (LoCoMo) / PARTIAL (LongMemEval) FOR FULL MIXING] must no longer be read as a category difference between benchmarks; the band assignment is a point classification on the frozen panel only. [BLOCK-ARM FRACTION NOT INTERPRETABLE ON LoCoMo] is strengthened, not weakened, and the bootstrap shows the block denominator is unstable on LongMemEval too (3,306 of 10,000 replicates carry a non-positive seed denominator there, against 8,910 and 8,410 on LoCoMo). The L-056 LoCoMo denominator-dispersion addendum is untouched by all of this: it concerns the full-Haar denominator of the boundary-localization line, not the block denominator.
proposed_narrow_conclusion_for_head_researcher_decision_not_adopted_here: "On a fixed pipeline, positive coordinate rescaling preserves the native sign representation exactly while raising retrieval score after full orthogonal mixing. The LoCoMo computation reproduced numerically from verified raw data across 92,100 question/seed/arm records. Denominator instability in the block-normalised comparisons, together with preregistration aggregation and interpretation inconsistencies, limits any comparative mechanism inference. Post-hoc sensitivity analyses do not repair that limit; they make it visible. This is not a general mechanism result, not a production claim, and does not generalise to other encoders or corpora."

The answer was already committed. docs/v52/task3/V52_T3A1_PROTOCOL_PATCH.md on main records "Dependency graph: 1 component(s), largest=470/470. question-level bootstrap cannot be interpreted as independent underlying-memory population inference." All 470 primary questions lie in ONE shared-session connected component. A cluster bootstrap needs at least two exchangeable clusters; with a single component it either returns the identical dataset in every replicate, carrying no information, or requires an arbitrary partition that violates the independence assumption the scheme exists to respect. The LongMemEval cluster bootstrap is therefore ILL-POSED, not merely undone, and must not be scheduled as pending work. What is genuinely absent is a different dependency-aware inference scheme for a single connected component, which is a design question rather than a re-run. The asymmetry with LoCoMo is explained rather than anomalous: LoCoMo's ten clusters came from producer archive-ordinal metadata, and ten is not one.

decision_1_accepted_with_limits: ACCEPTED - for LoCoMo the computational reproduction of the same implementation succeeded, and the scale-equalisation improvement on the frozen panel is preserved as a DESCRIPTIVE finding. NOT ACCEPTED - the comparative mechanism claim, and any population-level inference from the MOST/PARTIAL band labels. Recorded at the Head Researcher's explicit instruction: this decision does NOT mean "there is no mechanism".
decision_2_A_vs_B_ruled_from_the_preregistration_text_not_from_outcomes: The Head Researcher directed that the ruling be justified by the original preregistration text rather than by which value either definition produces, and the decision document's reasoning section contains no outcome value. Three textual facts in section 7 settle it. First, frac_arm is DEFINED under the governing clause "per rotation seed". Second, the decision bands are applied to "the seed-panel mean", i.e. the mean over seeds of the quantity just defined. Third, the denominators "must be reported with their per-seed dispersion", which presupposes a per-seed denominator not yet collapsed across seeds. Composing these gives: compute frac per seed, then average over seeds - definition A. Definition B requires averaging each arm across seeds BEFORE forming the ratio, an operation section 7 nowhere describes and which contradicts its per-seed clause. RULING: A is the preregistered estimand; B is preserved as the historical implementation output of the sealed runner and is not erased; both are always published together. No file change is needed for that, because the runner already persists frac_per_seed beside the seed-mean ratio.
correction_demanded_and_applied: my earlier statement that the A/B difference on the full arm is "within 0.001" is WITHDRAWN as a rounded paraphrase. Exact differences recomputed from the persisted summaries at 0c9916bd: LoCoMo full A-B = +0.0010792266983414844; LoCoMo block -0.2062003408892458; LongMemEval full +0.0006438693757745; LongMemEval block +0.0080525777340523. The correct statement is that the LoCoMo full-arm difference is approximately 0.001079 and that the divergence grows chiefly in the block arm. This is a presentational correction; it does not re-derive or reopen the accepted conclusion.
decision_3_section_7_and_9_conflict_recorded_as_an_explicit_deviation_and_NOT_resolved: section 7 demotes the interaction to secondary; section 9 nevertheless attaches the positive interpretation licence to that same secondary quantity, and "large I" is never defined anywhere in the document - section 7 supplies numeric bands for frac_arm (0.70 / 0.20) but supplies no band, threshold or direction test for I_frac. The conflict existed in the sealed text before any outcome was seen. It is recorded so it cannot later be closed by selecting whichever clause favours an observed result. No clause is selected.

NOT DONE IN THIS STAGE: the corpus was not read, the haystack_session_ids sharing graph was not rebuilt, its components were not recounted, and the "1 component, largest 470/470" figures were not re-derived. Those figures are INHERITED from Task 3A.1 with the standing that stage gave them. Confirming that a file contains a number is not confirming the number. A required citation label is added: [LONGMEMEVAL SINGLE-COMPONENT FINDING - INHERITED FROM TASK 3A.1; PROVENANCE VERIFIED, NOT RECOMPUTED HERE]. An independent recomputation would be a separate corpus-reading task and is neither proposed nor authorized.
closure_audit_prompt_prepared_not_launched: Scope is exactly the five items the Head Researcher named - the acceptance document, the A/B justification, the section 7/9 deviation, the source bindings, and current-state consistency - with gates C1 to C8. Everything else is closed at the edges IN WRITING: no re-run of any completed experiment, bootstrap or reproduction; no corpus read; no recomputation of the LongMemEval dependency graph, since its inherited status is itself one of the things under audit; no Task 4F1 action; no broad literature scan; no reopening of the accepted conclusion. The auditor is told to hash raw Git blobs rather than checked-out files, because two earlier auditor sessions reported mismatches that were most likely Windows CRLF checkout artifacts and neither confirmed it before being stopped. C2 explicitly invites a contrary reading: if the auditor thinks B is defensible from the preregistration text, that is a finding and not a failure. C7 asks the auditor to judge whether the one deliberate in-place state-file change - the stale key rename at cf03007, made in its own disclosed commit - was adequately disclosed or should have been additive. NO AGENT WAS LAUNCHED; commissioning is a Head Researcher decision and this session is the implementer and cannot audit its own acceptance record.
next_question_proposal_brought_as_asked: PROPOSAL ONLY, not a preregistration, not sealed, not authorized, no seeds drawn and no runner written. Question: after coordinate scales are equalised, does the spectral 32/64 split still beat a matched-size random split? It targets the one audited-established effect in the line (membership matters) with the one channel now supported descriptively (scale), and it avoids the defect that closed the last stage: the primary quantity is a DIFFERENCE between arms that are 8.448609 pp (LoCoMo) and 13.203121 pp (LongMemEval) apart with non-overlapping per-seed rho ranges, not a ratio over a near-zero block loss. Both figures were re-derived here from the committed boundary summaries at research 0c9916bd rather than quoted. The design fixes two known defects prospectively: the estimand is defined per seed and then averaged, applying the A ruling in the text before anything runs; and the interpretation licence is attached to the primary quantity with frozen numeric bands, which is what section 7 and section 9 failed to do.
expected_information_gain_stated_both_ways: if the gap survives rescaling, coordinate scale is NOT the channel behind the membership effect, which would separate two distinct damage channels and point positively at the correlation-structure and subspace-alignment candidates named in section 9 rather than merely failing to exclude them. If the gap collapses, one channel plausibly accounts for both findings, which unifies them but WEAKENS the independence of the audited membership result and any future write-up must carry that. Anything between narrows the share on a scale that cannot be floor-confounded. No outcome would identify the mechanism, establish necessity or exclusivity, claim production benefit, or transfer to other encoders.
the_alternative_i_declined_to_propose_first_and_why: the largest standing threat to this line is not a wrong mechanism but an effect specific to one representation - both benchmarks share a single TF-IDF/SVD pipeline, so the cross-benchmark agreement is not an independent methodological replication. A different-encoder replication tests that directly and is the higher-value experiment; it is not proposed first only because it needs a new frozen pipeline and new adapters and can reuse no validated control. If the Head Researcher would rather spend the next effort on generalisation than on mechanism, that is the question to ask and this proposal should be set aside rather than run first.
prohibitions_observed: no completed experiment, bootstrap or LoCoMo reproduction was repeated; no broad literature scan was restarted; no stopped agent was relaunched; no Task 4F1 run, finalize, HMAC or outcome authority was created or implied; no frozen file was edited.
outcome_boundary: Unchanged. Task 4F1 scientific preregistration SEALED at Seal V3; run BLOCKED; production authorization NONE; retrieval-quality outcome access FORBIDDEN. No BEAM corpus, candidate, seal, authorization or HMAC material was read or modified.
status: PASS
next_single_action: Head Researcher decision on two independent items: (a) commission the prepared narrow closure audit, in a session that did not write the acceptance record, and say whether this session may launch it or whether an external operator should; (b) rule on the next research question - take up the section 1 proposal, prefer the encoder-generalisation alternative, or neither. Task 4F1 stays on hold. D11 Drive backup still needs an operator with Drive access, and the GitHub default branch remains an owner-only setting.
