Checked against the audit's own evidence/c4b_exact_rational.json at 16e95a80, it does not hold. That file measures the absolute difference between each printed document value and its exact rational value, so it mixes float64 computation error with my own decimal truncation when typing the decision document, and is not a clean estimator-stability measurement in either direction. In FOUR CELLS OF FOUR, B's error is the LARGER one - LoCoMo full 2.542e-16 against 2.858e-16, LoCoMo block 9.682e-15 against 1.102e-14, LongMemEval full 2.108e-16 against 6.345e-16, LongMemEval block 4.723e-16 against 7.270e-15. So "A is less numerically stable than B, confirmed by exact arithmetic" is NOT SUPPORTED, and the audit's own F8 narrative draws an A-versus-B conclusion from a table that compares ARMS rather than estimators. That is a real inconsistency between the audit's narrative and its evidence; per the task it is reported narrowly and NOT adjudicated, and no new audit or experiment chain is opened.
the_magnitude_was_wrong_twice: pooling both estimators, the block-versus-full arm gap is 1.102e-14 against 6.345e-16, which is 1.24 ORDERS of magnitude, about 17x - not three orders. My Turkish relay compounded it by rendering "three orders" as "uc kat", three times.
what_survives_of_E8_and_on_what_evidence: the conceptual point stands but on different evidence. Averaging ten ratios whose denominators change sign within their own envelope is a fragile construction, and that rests on deviation-register D4 - LoCoMo block per-seed denominators from -0.0066 to +0.0164 and per-seed fractions from -3.40 to +2.64 - not on the audit's error table. The DIRECTION survives as well, because the fragility is a property of the block-arm denominator rather than of the choice between estimators, so it strengthens the refusal of the comparative claim whichever estimator is primary. NO ESTIMATOR RULING CHANGES: A remains the preregistered estimand on the textual grounds of section 7, which never depended on numerical stability, and estimator fragility is not evidence that a mechanism is absent.
the_codex_handover_is_superseded_on_this_point_only: docs/v52/V52_CLAUDE_TO_CODEX_HANDOVER_2026-09-07.md repeats the unsupported phrasing. It is hash-bound and is NOT edited; the decision note and this entry carry the correction additively, per the rule recorded at L-062.
the_decision_note_itself: compares Option A, mechanism decomposition inside the current representation, against Option B, generalisability across representation methods, answering for each the nine questions the task set - the open question addressed, what makes it not a repeat, what positive, negative and ambiguous outcomes would and would not license, the minimum viable design, which artifacts are reusable and which must be new, cost across code, data, model, memory, time and money, the basis and uncertainty of those estimates, and the independent controls and permissions required.
recommendation_and_the_reason_it_runs_against_the_stated_leaning: Option A is recommended as the single next step and Option B is deferred, explicitly NOT because it is less valuable - on value alone B is the more important question and the programme's leaning toward it is well founded. B is deferred because it is NOT YET WELL POSED. The effect under study is defined over VARIANCE-ORDERED axes: the frozen bases build the representation by hstacking word and char TF-IDF with an LSA block, reducing to 96 dimensions with TruncatedSVD, and the bands are High32 0:32, Mid32 32:64, Low32 64:96 over those components. A different representation must therefore either KEEP a reduction step, in which case a positive result is consistent with the effect being a property of variance-ordered truncation rather than of the encoder, or DROP it, in which case the axes carry no ordering, the 32/64 boundary has no referent, and the membership and spectral-position findings cannot even be posed. That fork is a written design decision to take before any compute. Option A's outcome also determines what B should test - scale heterogeneity if scale accounts for the membership gap, axis ordering if it does not - so running B first means choosing its target by guess.
cost_asymmetry_stated_with_its_basis_and_its_uncertainty: Option A's estimate is ANCHORED to this repository's own recorded timings, the 59.309 s LoCoMo invocation in the reproduction run receipt and the triggered run's persistence at 07:07:20Z and 07:11:15Z against a 07:06:19Z trigger, with identical arm count, seed count and cohorts, so the dominant cost driver is unchanged and the residual uncertainty is queueing rather than computation. Option B has NO comparable recorded run in this repository, needs a new adapter with its own leakage audit, NEW FROZEN ANCHORS - which means no native-reproduction abort exists to prove the pipeline is the audited one until they are established - and a pinned model download; whether it fits GitHub Actions limits is genuinely unknown and would need a feasibility check that was deliberately NOT performed here.
override_condition_and_a_third_answer_offered: if the near-term goal is external validity for a write-up rather than mechanism, B is the right spend and A can wait, but B should then begin with the scoping decision and a feasibility check rather than an experiment, both of which are cheap and need no compute authorization. Deferring BOTH is also admissible: nothing in the record is unstable, and the open items that need no experiment are owned elsewhere.
design_cautions_written_into_the_note: primary estimand and aggregation order fixed in the text before anything runs, per seed then seed-panel mean, matching the A ruling prospectively; the primary quantity is a DIFFERENCE between arms that are 8.448609 pp and 13.203121 pp apart with non-overlapping per-seed rho ranges, never a ratio over a near-zero block loss - while stating that using a difference removes the near-zero-denominator problem specifically and does NOT automatically dissolve floor or ceiling effects; band cut points inherited from the already-frozen 0.20/0.70 scale rather than invented, with 0.60 rather than 0.70 because a difference of differences is noisier, and explicitly not fitted to any observed value since none exists for this contrast; pairing at question and rotation seed; question-level bootstrap on both benchmarks and conversation-clustered bootstrap on LoCoMo ONLY, with none on LongMemEval, cited as inherited and not recomputed; four separate decisions reported separately with nothing pooled; seeds treated as a nuisance factor and not as independent statistical units; and an explicit statement of which generalisation would be defensible, namely question-level on the frozen panels under one shared pipeline and nothing beyond.
outcome_boundary: Unchanged. Task 4F1 scientific preregistration SEALED at Seal V3; run BLOCKED; production authorization NONE; retrieval-quality outcome access FORBIDDEN. No corpus read, no model downloaded, no experiment or bootstrap run, no BEAM artifact touched.
status: PASS
next_single_action: Head Researcher decision against the note - Option A as recommended, Option B with a scoping decision and feasibility check first, or defer both. No experiment is authorized by this entry and none is proposed for immediate execution.

The document carries [DRAFT - NOT AUTHORIZED FOR EXECUTION] in its status line and repeats the limit in its header and in its closing section, and it states that its own author must not seal it and must not run it.
follow_up_status_disclosed_in_the_document_not_only_here: section 1 records that the design was written AFTER the coordinate-scale and boundary-localization results had been run, audited and read, and lists what was already known to the author - the membership gap and its per-seed spread on the 58001-58010 panel, the accepted descriptive finding about 1/sigma, that the scale rule was chosen partly on a synthetic pilot, and that block-normalised ratios misbehaved. It states plainly that this is not a blind design and must not be presented as one, and that the only unknown is the primary quantity itself, for which no value exists anywhere.
priority_argued_without_claiming_a_prerequisite: the draft does NOT claim this work is a precondition for the representation-generalisation question. It says the opposite - that question can be taken up independently at any time and is on its own merits the more important one. Priority is argued only on expected information gain per unit of PREPARATION COST: this question is currently unanswerable from the record, its entire control suite is already validated, and its preparation cost is close to zero because every component exists and has been audited.
order_of_operations_written_normatively_with_provenance_of_every_parameter: centre, then rescale, then rotate. The representation is fitted on the archive's memory units only and the query is transformed by the already-fitted objects, so nothing in it is fitted on queries, gold sets, answers or outcomes. D = diag(1/sigma) is learned from the archive's own centered representation, per archive, from archive content only, never from queries, gold sets, outcomes or another archive. B32 membership is fixed by the SVD variance order and therefore by archive content with no per-experiment fitting; RANDOM32 membership is fixed by a partition seed with NO data entering the choice. Scaling is applied BEFORE rotation and never between group selection and rotation, and because D is diagonal coordinate i remains coordinate i, so membership is defined on the same indices in scaled and unscaled arms. The draft states that any implementation reordering these steps implements a different experiment.
single_unambiguous_primary_estimand_with_no_division: per rotation seed, G_s is the unscaled membership gap and G_scaled_s the scaled one, and Delta_s is their difference; the primary quantity is the seed-panel MEAN of Delta_s in percentage points, with per-seed values reported. Aggregation order is fixed in the text before any run - per seed, then averaged - matching the ruling that the per-seed-then-average form is the preregistered aggregation, stated in advance rather than settled afterwards. Pairing is at question and rotation seed; scaled and unscaled partners share the identical rotation matrix, and B32 and RANDOM32 at the same seed share identical Q32 and Q64 asserted at maximum absolute difference exactly 0.0. Any ratio over the block loss, the quantity that approached zero in the closed stage, is explicitly barred as primary.
the_decision_scale_is_measured_in_this_run_not_inherited: bands apply to Delta_bar over G_bar with both measured in this run, G_bar being the membership gap and a different quantity from the block loss. Cut points -0.20 and -0.60 are inherited from the already-frozen 0.20/0.70 scale so the programme keeps one notion of little and most, with -0.60 deliberately looser than 0.70 because Delta is a difference of differences and therefore noisier; no value of Delta_bar or G_bar for this arm combination exists, so nothing can have been fitted to an observed result. Two preconditions are checked at run time on this run's own arms - G_s positive on every seed, and G_bar at least 2.0 pp - and if either fails the verdict is [WITHHELD - GAP SCALE UNSUITABLE] with Delta_bar reported descriptively and no band. The prior 8.448609 pp and 13.203121 pp came from a DIFFERENT seed panel and the draft states they are not a guarantee about this run, which is why 2.0 pp is a loose floor rather than a prediction.
interpretation_is_not_allowed_to_rest_on_a_sign_or_on_seed_ranges: the draft names three things that may not carry the verdict on their own or together - the sign of Delta_bar, whether per-seed ranges of two arms overlap, and a point estimate without its dispersion - and calls them point observations on one fixed panel. Resampling is a question-level paired bootstrap on both benchmarks plus a conversation-clustered bootstrap on LoCoMo ONLY, both labelled sensitivity analyses and not certified intervals, with the LoCoMo clustered bootstrap's TEN clusters named as a stated limitation to be reported with the number rather than a reason to omit it. No clustered bootstrap on LongMemEval, whose single-component finding is cited as inherited and provenance-verified rather than recomputed. Seeds are a nuisance factor and not independent statistical units, ten is a small panel, and the two benchmarks are reported separately and never pooled, with agreement between them described as consistency under one shared pipeline rather than independent replication.
the_E8_lesson_is_written_into_the_design_as_a_standing_separation: differences between a printed decimal and an exactly computed value, of the order 1e-14 in this programme's records, are a reporting-precision question. The draft states they are not evidence about estimator stability in EITHER direction - so the corrected table is not to be read as showing B worse than A any more than it showed A worse than B - and that they are never to be mixed into or substituted for resampling uncertainty. Both are reported; neither argues the other.
outcome_licences_bounded_in_both_directions: if the gap persists the licensed reading is only that the SPECIFIC intervention of section 5 is not by itself sufficient to account for the membership gap, and it is explicitly NOT licensed to conclude that axis ordering is operative, that two separate causal mechanisms exist, that coordinate scale plays no role since it could act through a route this intervention does not touch, or that any alternative is positively supported - elimination is not identification. If the gap shrinks the licensed reading is only that this rescaling accounts for most of the gap on the frozen panel, and it is NOT licensed to conclude a single mechanism or any identification, while the outcome would WEAKEN the independence of the audited membership result, a cost any write-up must carry. No outcome licenses a mechanism, necessity, exclusivity, a general claim about coordinate scale, production benefit, transfer to other encoders, or upgrading a [LEAD].
scope_resources_and_time_with_the_estimate_marked_as_an_expectation: two benchmarks by ten seeds by six arms, LoCoMo as one job and LongMemEval as ten shards plus an aggregate; no new corpus and no model; memory as the audited stages with the invariance check already narrowed away from the archive Gram matrix; time of the order of minutes rather than hours, stated as an EXPECTATION and NOT a guarantee, reasoned from identical arm count, seed count, cohorts and per-question workload rather than from a promise that a past duration repeats, with queueing, runner variance or an implementation difference able to move it and a factor of two either way not changing whether the experiment is worth running. The two runners and their tests are NOT written and writing them is not authorized by this draft.
seeds_are_fresh_by_design: rotation 60001-60010 and partition 70001-70010 paired by position, deliberately neither the audited 58001-58010 nor the coordinate-scale 59001-59010, so that no panel already known to show an effect is reused. No replacement seeds after outcome access.
gates_that_remain_ahead_each_a_separate_decision: design acceptance; implementation of the runners and tests in a non-triggerable location; independent review of that implementation; a pre-run seal binding every blob; exactly one trigger; and a cold-start independent audit afterwards by a session that did not prepare it.
outcome_boundary: Unchanged. Task 4F1 scientific preregistration SEALED at Seal V3; run BLOCKED; production authorization NONE; retrieval-quality outcome access FORBIDDEN. No corpus read, no model downloaded, no code written, no experiment, pilot, retrieval, fitting, ranking or bootstrap run.

### L-066

```text
timestamp_utc: 2026-09-07T14:06:00Z
actor_role: Continuity Lead / co-chair (sole writer on main)
predecessor_commit_or_tag: L-065 / 56c2941
scope: Answer the seven review points on the Option A draft with an additive design revision. Documentation only. No new code, no pilot, no bootstrap, no retrieval, no model or corpus download, no agent commissioned, no Task 4F1 action, and the closure audit is not reopened. The hash-bound draft is NOT edited.
changed_or_created_paths: docs/v52/V52_MEMBERSHIP_UNDER_SCALING_DESIGN_REVISION_R1_2026-09-07.md with sidecar (sha256 3d7a79b249e97a1bc2657a6782aec5e6a138f6a8c5e2681978ed66ad217b3391); ops/CURRENT_STATE.json; docs/CONTINUITY_LEDGER.md
how_the_seven_items_were_handled_honestly: three were already fully or partly specified and are answered by citing the section - item 7 by draft section 1, item 3's formulas by draft section 7, item 2's shared-Q control by draft section 5 step 3. Four were genuine gaps and are supplied: the scaling operator's estimator and query rule, the draw structure and pairing convention, the sign convention and mandatory joint reporting, and the decision rules. TWO SUBSTANTIVE DESIGN CHANGES were accepted from the review rather than defended, and both are improvements I did not see myself.
change_one_the_gate_could_have_suppressed_reporting_and_is_reformed: the draft's section 8 made the normalised reading conditional on two run-time checks and said the verdict would be WITHHELD, which is exactly the outcome-dependent door the review names. Revised so that all six arms, all ten seeds, every per-question record, Ghat, Ghat_scaled, Delta_bar, rho_hat with their per-seed values, all uncertainty output, all diagnostics and all control results are published UNCONDITIONALLY under every outcome. A gate may only decline to attach the CATEGORICAL VERDICT and may never trigger new seeds, a re-run, selective exclusion of seeds, archives or questions, or the withholding of any measurement.
change_one_continued_the_per_seed_positivity_requirement_is_withdrawn: the draft required G_k > 0 on every one of ten seeds. The review is right that one seed should not suspend an entire interpretation. It is replaced by a REPORTED DIAGNOSTIC - the count of seeds with G_k <= 0 - whose effect enters through the uncertainty interval rather than through a switch. If seeds disagree in sign about whether spectral beats random, that is information about the panel and must be visible rather than a reason to say nothing. One gate survives, on the relative scale only, because rho is a ratio and is meaningless when its denominator is near zero, which is the failure mode that ended the previous stage: if Ghat < 2.0 pp the categorical verdict is not attached and the absolute results are still reported. The 2.0 pp figure is now stated plainly as a JUDGMENT rather than a derivation - roughly a quarter of the smaller gap previously observed on a DIFFERENT seed panel, far enough above zero that rho is not dominated by its denominator, loose enough not to encode a prediction - and it is changeable now, before any run, not afterwards.
change_two_the_draft_had_no_category_for_the_gap_growing_or_reversing: with three bands, a positive Delta would have been classified as "does not account for the gap", which is true but hides a qualitatively different result. Five point categories now sit on rho_hat: at or below -1.00 DIRECTION REVERSED UNDER RESCALING, meaning random membership performs at least as well as spectral after rescaling; -1.00 to -0.60 ACCOUNTS FOR MOST; -0.60 to -0.20 PARTIAL; -0.20 to +0.20 ALONE DOES NOT ACCOUNT; above +0.20 MEMBERSHIP GAP LARGER UNDER RESCALING.
the_actual_decision_rule_is_the_uncertainty_overlay_not_the_point_estimate: if the question-level paired bootstrap interval for rho lies entirely inside one category, that category is the verdict; if it spans two or more, the verdict is INDETERMINATE naming the spanned categories, and the point classification is reported as a point observation with no verdict attached. This is what replaces deciding by the sign of Delta_bar or by whether per-seed ranges overlap, both of which stay barred. A naming discipline binds every category: no label may be restated as a mechanism having been explained, so accounting for most of the gap does not license "coordinate scale explains the membership effect" and failing to account for it does not license "axis ordering is the mechanism" or "there are two mechanisms".
the_scaling_operator_is_now_a_specification_rather_than_a_description: sigma uses ddof=0, the population form, stated because the sample form differs by sqrt(n/(n-1)) and would define a different intervention, and because ddof=0 matches the estimator already implemented and audited. Sigma is computed on the centered C actually used downstream, not on raw SVD output and not on singular values. eps=1e-12 is a FALLBACK and not a clip - a sub-eps coordinate keeps d=1, is never divided by eps and is never dropped, so the transform stays total and the coordinate stays in the sign code - and it is inherited rather than re-chosen, a guard against division by zero rather than a tuning parameter. The IDENTICAL D is applied to the query and nothing whatever is estimated from the query. Fallback counts, the >4-of-96 archive flag and CV(sigma) before and after are declared diagnostics and explicitly not decision inputs.
the_matching_is_now_countable: seeds are paired ONE-TO-ONE by position, rotation 60000+k with partition 70000+k, and explicitly NOT crossed - a crossed design would multiply cost tenfold and answer no question here, and fixing this now prevents it being chosen later. Per seed there is exactly one (Q32,Q64) Haar draw serving all four rotated arms and one permutation serving both random-membership arms. Across the four cells at a seed the questions, gold sets, tie priority, twenty nuisance trials, C, Q32 and Q64 are identical and only membership and the presence of D vary, so any difference is attributable to those two factors and to nothing drawn separately. Totals per benchmark: 10 Haar draws, 10 partitions, 40 rotated evaluations, and per-question rows of n x 10 x 6, that is 92,100 for LoCoMo and 28,200 for LongMemEval, matching the closed stage's schema so an auditor can rebuild every aggregate.
sign_convention_and_mandatory_joint_reporting: G > 0 means spectral beats random; Delta < 0 means the gap shrank and Delta > 0 that it grew; rho = 0 is no change, rho = -1 is the gap exactly eliminated and rho < -1 is a sign reversal. Ghat, Ghat_scaled, Delta_bar, rho_hat and all per-seed values are reported together in every outcome, because a change is uninterpretable without the level it changed from and the level it left behind.
a_sentence_in_my_own_draft_predicted_a_result_and_is_withdrawn: draft section 9 said the LoCoMo clustered interval "will be wide and unreliable". That forecasts an outcome before the run. Withdrawn and replaced: LoCoMo has ten conversation clusters, and a resampling estimate built on ten units has limited reliability; the interval's actual width is an outcome and will be reported, not forecast. The LongMemEval question-level bootstrap must not be presented as population inference, because the recorded single-component dependency - inherited from Task 3A.1 and provenance-verified rather than recomputed - means questions are not exchangeable.
uncertainty_mechanics_now_fully_specified: question-level resampling with replacement, 10,000 replicates, ONE resampled index set per replicate applied identically to all six arms and all ten seeds so every pairing survives inside each replicate, unweighted at question level, the SEED PANEL FIXED and not resampled with Delta_k recomputed per seed inside each replicate and then averaged over the fixed panel, percentile interval at 2.5 and 97.5, two datasets evaluated separately and never pooled with no cross-benchmark statistic defined, and a LoCoMo secondary clustered bootstrap that resamples clusters and carries all of their questions so it is question-weighted by construction.
the_representation_versus_sampling_separation_is_carried_into_the_design: printed-decimal versus exactly-computed differences of order 1e-14 are a reporting-precision matter, are evidence about estimator stability in NEITHER direction, and are never mixed into or substituted for the resampling intervals.
five_design_decisions_are_left_open_deliberately: the 2.0 pp floor; the four band cut points, of which -0.60 and -0.20 are inherited from the closed stage's scale while -1.00 and +0.20 are new and chosen for symmetry rather than derived; whether the LoCoMo clustered bootstrap is co-primary or secondary, where ten clusters is the reason for the current secondary treatment; whether the indeterminate overlay should require both schemes to agree on LoCoMo; and whether the remaining scaled gap deserves its own preregistered band, which would have to be added before sealing rather than after.
outcome_boundary: Unchanged. Task 4F1 scientific preregistration SEALED at Seal V3; run BLOCKED; production authorization NONE; retrieval-quality outcome access FORBIDDEN. No corpus read, no model downloaded, no code written, no experiment, pilot, retrieval, fitting, ranking or bootstrap run.
status: PASS
next_single_action: Head Researcher review of revision R1 together with the bound draft, and a decision on the five open design questions. Nothing is accepted, sealed, implemented or authorized to run, and this session must not seal or run it.
```

changed_or_created_paths: docs/v52/V52_MEMBERSHIP_UNDER_SCALING_DESIGN_REVISION_R2_2026-09-07.md with sidecar (sha256 39cbfcea3a93307d582f9765529d6d99c09d0204706befa6470b8b932f451669); ops/CURRENT_STATE.json; docs/CONTINUITY_LEDGER.md
the_criticism_is_correct_and_i_am_not_defending_the_design: this line began by replacing a ratio with a difference, precisely because a ratio over a near-zero denominator is what made the previous stage's block arm uninterpretable. R1 then REINTRODUCED a ratio, rho = Delta_bar over G_bar, for no reason other than to attach a categorical verdict - and that single choice pulled in a denominator to protect, a 2.0 pp floor to protect it with, and four cut points to divide it. rho is NOT the same quantity as the old block-loss ratio and would not automatically have been as fragile, so the objection is not that it repeats the old error. The objection is that the question never needed it: the complexity was in the labelling, not in the science.
what_the_experiment_now_answers_with_three_numbers: G_bar the grouping gap before the intervention, G_bar_scaled the gap remaining after it, and Delta_bar the paired change, all in percentage points, all reported together with every per-seed value and an interval for each. Nothing else is a deliverable.
a_dispute_that_removing_the_ratio_dissolves_rather_than_decides: because Delta is LINEAR in the arm means, the mean of the per-seed differences equals the difference of the seed means EXACTLY, not approximately. The A-versus-B aggregation question that consumed the closed stage and produced the section 7 ruling was a consequence of the estimand being a RATIO. With a difference there is nothing to rule on. Simplifying therefore removes that entire class of dispute from this experiment instead of deciding it again.
the_full_withdrawal_list_every_item_of_which_was_mine: rho as the primary decision quantity; the five categorical labels; the cut points -1.00, -0.60, -0.20 and +0.20; the 2.0 pp floor on G_bar, which existed only to protect rho's denominator and goes with it; the [RELATIVE SCALE UNSUITABLE] state, which no longer has anything to guard; the [INDETERMINATE - INTERVAL SPANS ...] overlay as a VERDICT MECHANISM, the interval now being reported directly rather than converted into a label; the three-band decision rule of draft section 8; a preregistered band on the remaining gap, which R1 had raised as an open question and which is DECLINED; and the per-seed positivity gate, already withdrawn in R1 and staying withdrawn.
the_deliberate_consequence: this study produces an ESTIMATE, not a verdict. There is no preregistered threshold and no categorical output, and that is the intended design rather than an omission.
the_loophole_that_removing_thresholds_could_have_opened_is_closed_explicitly: because no threshold is preregistered, NO threshold may be introduced after outcome access - not to declare "most", not "little", not equivalence, not under any other name. The no-tuning rule is restated in R2 so that it cannot be read as covering only seeds and parameters.
the_ratio_is_not_banned_it_is_simply_not_a_deliverable: it is not computed, not reported and not part of the preregistered output. Anyone who later wants it can derive it from the three reported numbers without re-running anything; that derivation needs no new design and opens no audit chain, which is why no such chain is proposed.
locomo_resampling_targets_weights_and_limits_are_now_written_separately: the question-level scheme targets variability under question exchangeability, is unweighted, and DOES NOT ACCOUNT FOR WITHIN-CONVERSATION DEPENDENCE AT ALL, so its interval is narrower than the dependence structure warrants. The conversation-cluster scheme targets variability under conversation exchangeability, carries all questions of a selected conversation and is therefore question-weighted by construction, and is built on TEN units with correspondingly limited reliability - whose width is an outcome and is deliberately not forecast. Stated because it is easy to get backwards: the existence of ten clusters does NOT make the question-level analysis more trustworthy about dependence; it is the reason that analysis is inadequate on that axis.
the_two_schemes_are_not_independent_confirmation: they are two views of the same fixed panel under different exchangeability assumptions, computed from the same records, so agreement between them is not corroboration. If they diverge, the divergence IS the finding: both intervals are reported together with the sentence that the conclusion is sensitive to the choice of resampling unit, neither is promoted over the other, and no reconciled single interval is manufactured.
longmemeval_stays_a_sensitivity_analysis_and_its_inherited_finding_stays_labelled: question-level resampling only, never presented as population inference, because the 470 primary questions lie in a single shared-session component - a finding INHERITED from Task 3A.1 and provenance-verified, NOT recomputed here, and carrying its label wherever it is relied on. No conversation-cluster bootstrap is defined for LongMemEval.
the_reporting_sentence_and_the_prohibitions_are_fixed_before_any_run: the sentence states the gap before, the gap remaining, the paired change and the intervals, under the named intervention and on the frozen panel. Prohibited in any outcome: calling a change a causal mechanism or saying the effect has been explained; generalising beyond the frozen panels, this pipeline or this specific intervention; concluding "there is no effect", INCLUDING from an interval that spans zero; claiming practical equivalence; introducing any threshold, band or label after outcome access; and upgrading a [LEAD]. An interval spanning zero is reported as exactly that - the data on this panel do not resolve the sign of the change - and is not evidence of absence and must not be converted into one.
what_r2_leaves_untouched: the six arms; the matched draw structure with seeds paired one-to-one and not crossed; the scaling operator specification including ddof=0, the eps fallback and the identical D applied to the query with nothing estimated from it; unconditional publication of all arms, seeds, per-question records, aggregates, diagnostics and controls under every outcome; the mandatory control suite; the no-tuning rule; the outcome-licence limits; and the follow-up framing that fresh seeds on previously examined corpora do not restore blindness.
outcome_boundary: Unchanged. Task 4F1 scientific preregistration SEALED at Seal V3; run BLOCKED; production authorization NONE; retrieval-quality outcome access FORBIDDEN. No corpus read, no model downloaded, no code written, no experiment, pilot, retrieval, fitting, ranking or bootstrap run.
status: PASS

Sealing and execution on real data were NOT accepted and remain unauthorized.
a_normative_index_now_exists_so_an_implementer_cannot_resurrect_withdrawn_rules: three documents describe this design and stay separate because corrections are additive, so the binding record states precedence R2 then R1 then draft, names which section of each is superseded, and lists what is DEAD AND FORBIDDEN in the implementation - any ratio of Delta to G under any name, the four cut points, the five categorical labels, the 2.0 pp floor and its RELATIVE SCALE UNSUITABLE state, the INDETERMINATE overlay as a verdict mechanism, the three-band rule, any band on the remaining gap, and the per-seed positivity gate.
clarification_one_a_direction_i_asserted_without_establishing_it: R2 said the question-level interval "is narrower than the dependence structure warrants". WITHDRAWN. The binding replacement is that the scheme does not model within-conversation dependence and may therefore UNDERSTATE uncertainty, with no claim about the interval's width in either direction before it is computed.
clarification_two_the_panel_estimate_and_the_resampling_envelope_are_separated: the point estimate on the fixed panel is a computed number whose sign is known and does not become unknown because an interval is wide; an interval spanning zero means both directions are supported under resampling and the interpretation is unresolved. It is NOT evidence of absence and NOT a claim of practical equivalence. Both are reported together and neither erases the other.
clarification_three_the_cluster_algorithm_is_bound_literally_because_question_weighting_is_not_automatic: per replicate, draw n_clusters conversations WITH REPLACEMENT, take ALL questions of each draw, PRESERVE MULTIPLICITY so a conversation drawn twice contributes its questions twice, concatenate into the replicate's question multiset, divide by the total number of selected question SLOTS rather than by distinct questions or by clusters, and apply the same selection to all six arms and to the fixed ten-seed panel. A different algorithm must be declared BEFORE implementation rather than discovered afterwards in the code.
clarification_four_a_ratio_derived_later_has_no_standing: if anyone computes one from the three reported numbers at any later time it is not a deliverable, not a preregistered result and not part of this record, and it may not silently alter the scientific statement; changing that statement requires a new decision taken before the new quantity is looked at.
clarification_five_what_linearity_does_and_does_not_require: the implementation must preserve the same question scope and the same weights on both sides of the identity, and must NOT require bitwise equality of the two computations, because floating-point summation is order-dependent in its last digits. The consistency check is a TOLERANCE check at 1e-12, and an implementation asserting exact equality would be wrong and would fail for a reason unrelated to the science.
the_implementation_is_prepared_and_i_may_not_call_it_reviewed: core and tests live in drafts/v52/membership_impl_2026_09_07/ on a separate branch, core sha256 707e166c80d9231d089341a804e0acdd8845781fec7560821c67b3f6d0731a00 and tests sha256 97e6e7206864dffe4638a19231bcc5ee706cda2906cf08890ac80aa7e1cd279f. There is NO workflow file and the directory is not research/v52/, so nothing can fire by push, schedule or dispatch. REAL_DATA_EXECUTION_ENABLED is False and the gate raises unless explicitly overridden; the module reads no corpus, downloads nothing and imports nothing that does.
seventy_four_synthetic_checks_all_pass_and_three_things_are_demonstrated_rather_than_asserted: percentage-point units and slot-weighted aggregation, where arm fractions 0.30 minus 0.22 give exactly 8.0 pp and a repeated index counts twice; PAIRING, where a per-question change fixed at -5 pp yields a degenerate interval of exactly [-5.000000, -5.000000] while the level G still varies across replicates - a result independent per-arm resampling could not produce; and CLUSTER MULTIPLICITY, where drawing cluster A twice returns exactly 1.0 pp over 6 slots and drawing A then B returns the question-weighted 3/8 rather than the cluster-averaged 0.5.
the_refusals_are_exercised_not_promised: records that are missing, duplicated, NaN, out of range, unknown-arm, unknown-seed or malformed each raise; result files are never overwritten silently; and the matched-Q, identity, orthogonality, invariance, partition and linearity checks are each shown able to fail. The forbidden apparatus is asserted absent from the executable code by the test suite rather than by a comment, with the two legitimate uses of the word "threshold" - the diagnostic counter and the inherited zero-threshold sign quantization - enumerated rather than banned.
a_defect_this_preparation_found_in_itself_and_disclosed: DEGENERATE_FLAG_THRESHOLD was DEAD CODE standing in for a design requirement. The code recorded the degenerate-coordinate count but never applied the per-archive FLAG that R1 section 1 requires, so a requirement existed only as an unused constant. Fixed by returning a diagnostics dictionary from scale_matrix carrying the count AND the flag together, so that a caller cannot record one and forget the other, with tests that the flag fires above 4 and does not fire at 3. Disclosed here and in README_IMPL.md rather than quietly corrected, and the crude self-check that missed it was tightened rather than deleted.
what_is_not_validated: anything requiring a corpus. No pipeline has been executed end to end, no real retrieval computed and no real-data bootstrap run. The corpus-bound runner is NOT written, because writing it is not authorized at this stage. The tests ran on this machine's Python and NumPy rather than the pinned research stack; the functions do not depend on the version, but that is an inference and not a run.
outcome_boundary: Unchanged. Task 4F1 scientific preregistration SEALED at Seal V3; run BLOCKED; production authorization NONE; retrieval-quality outcome access FORBIDDEN.
status: PASS
next_single_action: Head Researcher decision on commissioning an INDEPENDENT IMPLEMENTATION REVIEW of the pushed branch, by a session that did not write it. This session prepared the implementation and cannot review it. Nothing is sealed, and real-data execution remains off by default and unauthorized.
```

the_review_also_demonstrated_the_crlf_hazard_rather_than_asserting_it: the acceptance record's blob is a99d547d while the same file checked out on a Windows clone hashes 91380902, which is why every prompt in this programme now instructs auditors to hash raw Git blobs.
two_blockers_and_both_were_mine: F-1, cv_sigma_after was the standard deviation of the post-scaling sigma and never divided by its mean, while cv_sigma_before WAS a genuine CV - on a 20-degenerate-coordinate archive it reported 0.406116 where the true CV is 0.512989, and R1 section 4 publishes every diagnostic unconditionally, so a wrong number under a wrong name would have entered the record. F-2, cluster labels were checked only for LENGTH, so a NaN label produced a cluster whose member set was EMPTY - because labels == nan is false everywhere - and every replicate drawing it silently removed those questions from the numerator AND from the slot denominator; a three-cluster draw covering all ten questions produced n_question_slots = 8, and mixed-type labels split one conversation in two.
how_F1_was_fixed_within_the_stated_constraints: cv_sigma_after is now a genuine CV computed by a shared helper, so the two diagnostics cannot drift apart again. NO epsilon is added to the denominator and the definition is not silently changed: a zero mean returns NaN and that case is tested on an all-zero archive, which is the only way a non-negative sigma vector can have a zero mean. The quantity v1 published is not dropped - it is still reported, under its true name sd_sigma_after - so the record loses nothing and gains an honest label.
how_F2_was_fixed_and_the_distinction_the_task_asked_for: labels are validated BEFORE any grouping. None, NaN, bool and unsupported types are rejected with named errors, and MIXED kinds are rejected rather than coerced, because coercion would either merge 1 with "1" into one conversation or split one conversation into two. Every cluster must be non-empty, and the partition is PROVED rather than assumed: every question in exactly one cluster and the total membership equal to the question count. The two things that were previously confusable are now separate diagnostics - questions_lost_to_invalid_labels, which is structurally zero because validation precedes grouping, and mean_clusters_not_drawn_per_replicate, which is ordinary resampling and carries a note saying so.
the_other_findings_closed: F-3 duplicate question ids raise a named DesignViolation instead of an IndexError; F-6 the real-data gate reads the module global at CALL time, so flipping the constant now genuinely opens it, tested by flipping and restoring; F-7 and F-8 index selections are validated for integer dtype and range, so negative indices no longer wrap and out-of-range raises a DesignViolation rather than an IndexError; F-9 a boolean score is rejected explicitly; F-10 an archive of fewer than two rows is rejected; F-11 the no-op body.replace("block",""), the dead "distinct" if False else "partition" conditional and the vacuous spans_zero in (True, False) are gone, the last replaced by asserting is True on a fixture centred at zero and is False on one far from it.
F_12_deliberately_not_acted_on: binding section 6 fixes the linearity tolerance at an ABSOLUTE 1e-12 and says a relative form would need a new decision. The review's measured worst case - 5.116e-14, about 19.5x headroom - is recorded in a module constant and nowhere acted on.
F_4_was_answered_by_dropping_a_claim_rather_than_patching_a_scan: the reviewer added a genuine Delta/G ratio named relative_change and ALL 74 of my checks still passed, so the forbidden-token scan was never assurance. v2 does not repeat that claim. A word scan cannot see a quantity spelled differently, and AST enumeration - though far stronger - is explicitly stated NOT to be a proof that no ratio can ever be introduced. The primary guarantee is now the CONFORMANCE test, which recomputes the three reported quantities from an independent reference implementation written from the design text in plain Python and requires exact agreement, together with a CLOSED output schema compared against a frozen expected set. That schema check is what kills the relative_change mutation: a quantity added under any name fails a test instead of slipping past a scan.
F_5_fixed_by_replacing_the_fixture_not_the_wording: the v1 cluster fixture gave identical values under slot-weighting and distinct-question weighting, so it could not demonstrate the bound denominator while the README said it did. v2 uses three clusters of unequal size and value on which slot-, distinct- and cluster-weighting give three different answers, and asserts the core matches the slot value and differs from the other two. The README claims now match what is actually tested.
evidence_and_the_scope_of_the_kill_count: 88 synthetic checks, ALL PASS. 16 mutation probes re-anchored from the reviewer's script with attribution to its commit, 15 killed. Reported strictly as the task requires: this shows the suite catches exactly these 16 fault variants and is NOT evidence that the core is free of defects.
the_one_survivor_is_expected_and_is_not_a_hole: M16 removes the partition-coverage guard, which is UNREACHABLE through the public API because label validation precedes it and every question therefore carries exactly one label. A mutation test cannot kill an unreachable guard. That is a property of defence in depth, and it is reported as such rather than removed, and rather than papered over with a test that reaches it only by monkeypatching internals.
a_defect_in_my_own_probe_disclosed_rather_than_quietly_corrected: my FIRST re-anchoring of the reviewer's M3 computed the cluster average into an UNUSED variable, so it mutated nothing and reported a FALSE hole in my own suite. I repaired the probe to replace the assignment that is actually used, after which M3 is killed. Both the error and the repair are recorded in the script, because a probe that cannot mutate is worse than no probe - it manufactures reassurance in the opposite direction.
a_process_incident_i_caused_and_contained: a blanket __pycache__ cleanup in the v2 worktree deleted a TRACKED .pyc file inside task4f1_execution_candidate_2026_08_31, a Task 4F1 namespace. Bitwise equality is deliberately NOT demanded: floating-point summation is order-dependent in its last digits and a bit-level requirement here would be an unjustified new condition. Measured agreement on the fixture is max|diff| = 3.55e-15 per vector. Two negative controls assert the comparison rejects a perturbed scalar and a single perturbed element of a per-seed vector. A machine sweep of the suite's own AST reports any check whose condition is a boolean expression ending in a literal True; it reports none, and carries a control proving the sweep detects that shape when present. SCOPE STATED RATHER THAN IMPLIED: that sweep covers that ONE shape and nothing else, and no claim of comprehensive test soundness is made.

changed_or_created_paths: ops/CURRENT_STATE.json; docs/CONTINUITY_LEDGER.md; and on impl/v52-membership-runner-v1-2026-09-08 only (commits 0c526255 and a7ecc496): drafts/v52/membership_runner_v1_2026_09_08/{membership_runner.py, synthetic_adapter.py, test_membership_runner.py, RUNNER_SPEC.md, GOVERNING_AND_LINEAGE.md, SCOPE_NOTE_FOR_INDEPENDENT_REVIEW.md, ENVIRONMENT_LOCK_PROPOSAL.md, evidence/*}
an_omission_in_my_own_record_corrected_additively: the closure check at 712412a5 raised FIVE new items - NEW-1, NEW-2, NEW-3, NEW-4 and NEW-5 - and L-070 and L-071 carried only the first four. NEW-5 is recorded here for the first time: "integer labels are keyed by int()", so two labels that are distinct objects but equal under int() are merged into one cluster, the realistic instance being members of two different IntEnum classes sharing a value, which give n_clusters = 1. Like NEW-4 it was classed an OBSERVATION, not a defect against F-2's wording. Earlier entries are not edited; this entry is the correction.
what_is_closed_and_what_is_not_stated_separately: CLOSED and not to be reopened - F-1 through F-12 (closed at 712412a5) and NEW-1, NEW-2, NEW-3 (closed at aa0ee8a9, CLOSURE PASS). NOT CLOSED - NEW-4 and NEW-5, both answered in this delivery at the INGESTION boundary only with the core left byte-unchanged, so the observations against the core stand; and O-1, W-1, W-2, which are deliberately untouched backlog. "All findings are closed" is not an accurate sentence about this programme and is not written anywhere.
why_NEW_4_and_NEW_5_had_to_be_answered_before_the_runner: they are harmless in the core and dangerous at ingestion. LoCoMo cluster labels are conversation ids read from CSV or JSON, and a missing id renders as exactly one of the strings the core accepts as an ordinary label. The wording matters and was corrected: it is NOT true that a visible eleventh cluster necessarily appears. Several missing ids collapse into ONE pseudo-conversation, a missing id equal to an existing one is absorbed, and an int()-merged pair REDUCES the count. The accurate statement is that the grouping can be corrupted SILENTLY with the total unchanged, and that is why a count check is not accepted as evidence anywhere in this runner.
how_the_ingestion_boundary_was_built: identifiers are STRINGS ONLY, narrower than the core on purpose - accepting no integer makes the int() merge of NEW-5 unreachable rather than merely tested, and a source with integer ids must render them as strings in the adapter where the choice is visible. Designated missing-value indicators - the empty string, whitespace-only, "nan", "None", "null", "na", "n/a", "nil", "-", "--", "?" - are rejected by name, detected on a case-folded stripped COPY so the value itself is never modified. A leading- or trailing-space id is REJECTED rather than stripped, because stripping would silently merge " c1" with "c1", and the instruction was that valid identifiers are never silently altered or merged. A valid id is returned byte-identical; "nancy-01", which merely contains a rejected token, passes untouched.
the_source_identity_contract_does_not_accept_a_count: six checks against a hash-bound expected-mapping manifest - identifier validation, duplicate question ids, missing ids, extra ids, the PER-QUESTION conversation mapping, and conversation id SET equality. The negative tests include three corruptions that leave the total unchanged and must still be caught: two questions swapped between conversations, one whole conversation relabelled, and one conversation's ids all rendered as the empty string.
the_expected_mapping_is_MISSING_and_was_not_invented: no document bound to this experiment supplies a conversation-id inventory or a question-to-conversation map. All four normative documents were searched; the acceptance record's section 4 says only "draw n_clusters conversations". The runner therefore treats the manifest as a REQUIRED EXTERNAL INPUT, hash-bound at load time, and the contract is demonstrated against the synthetic adapter's known mapping. A candidate artifact exists at audit_v52_t4f0_codex_2026_08_31/conversation_inventory.csv but belongs to a different task's audit namespace, nothing binds it to this experiment, and its contents were deliberately NOT read. Binding it is a Head Researcher decision.
N_2_discharged: the bootstrap seed is written to a NEW file through the core's refusal-to-overwrite path BEFORE any result exists, and reading that record is the only way a run obtains a seed. A record written for a different (benchmark, scheme) is refused, so a record cannot be reused across arms, a seed cannot be chosen after seeing a result, and a second seed cannot be tried into the same record. The seed record's own sha256 travels into the output. The seed VALUES remain a preregistration decision and are not chosen here.
N_3_discharged: the LongMemEval inheritance tag is emitted in every LongMemEval output and states the structure (single connected component over all 470 questions), the provenance (inherited from Task 3A.1, provenance-verified, NOT recomputed), the consequence (the conversation-cluster bootstrap is ILL-POSED, not merely wide) and the authority (R2 line 141). Requesting the cluster scheme for LongMemEval raises a named violation. LoCoMo outputs carry no tag, so it can never be read as applying to both.
N_4_discharged: the centering vector and D are learned from the ARCHIVE alone, and the query is asserted to have been transformed by the IDENTICAL objects, BITWISE. Bitwise is right here where tolerance is right in the core: this asserts that the same numbers were reused, not that two summation orders agree, and a tolerance would admit a query mean that merely resembles the archive mean, which is the failure N-4 names. The synthetic query is drawn from a deliberately different distribution, so the test would fail if the transform were refitted; a 1-ULP perturbation of D is rejected.
the_environment_lock_for_this_experiment_DOES_NOT_EXIST: all four normative documents were searched and none states a Python version, a package version, or a determinism or thread policy. The acceptance record's section 7 authorizes runner preparation and synthetic tests and says nothing about an execution environment. The Task 4F1 V7 lock was therefore NOT applied to this follow-up; it belongs to a different, sealed and blocked task and is quoted only as the nearest precedent.
what_was_actually_used_and_how_it_is_labelled: Python 3.12.14, numpy 2.3.2, scipy 1.16.1, scikit-learn 1.7.1, psutil 7.0.0 on Windows-10-10.0.19045, with PYTHONHASHSEED=0 and all four thread variables at 1, measured rather than assumed. Against the 4F1 V7 lock the numerical stack matches exactly and Python differs by ONE PATCH RELEASE - that lock says 3.12.13. No package was installed, no interpreter changed, no existing lock edited and NO FINAL LOCK SELECTED. 85 runner checks and 99 core regression checks pass there, and both results are labelled DEVELOPMENT-ENVIRONMENT SYNTHETIC TESTS: not evidence of lock conformance, not a seal, and not authorization to run. Full environment verification stays OPEN, and a two-option proposal is put to the Head Researcher rather than resolved.
what_is_NOT_claimed: no claim is made that any wrong ingestion is necessarily caught. What is demonstrated is the declared input schema, the named rejection of each identifier shape, the six-check identity contract including a corruption that leaves the cluster count unchanged, N-2, N-3, N-4, and that the output schema, the refusal to overwrite and the real-data gate survive integration. The runner is prepared by the Continuity Lead and may NOT be called independently reviewed.
the_real_data_path_is_a_refusing_stub_by_design: run_on_real_corpus calls the core's authorization check and then refuses, and there is nothing behind it. Even with the core flag flipped it still refuses, because no ingestion path exists to authorize. A stub that refuses is honest where an unreachable implementation would invite a later reader to flip a flag and believe it had been reviewed.
namespaces_kept_separate_as_required: the runner branch is based on the CLOSED candidate v3 at dcb568d0, so the audited core is present byte-unchanged at bc2282d3fccfe83c3e9fc36a59d7df8e4f748048ff94baebdfa4010584404e72 and importable. The two commits add exactly 10 files, all under drafts/v52/membership_runner_v1_2026_09_08/, and touch nothing in the v1, v2 or v3 candidate namespaces or in any audit namespace.
remaining_before_real_data: a bound expected-mapping manifest per benchmark; an approved environment lock; the registered bootstrap seed VALUES; a real ingestion path, which is not authorized; an independent review of this runner, which is NOT commissioned - its scope note is written and waiting; and a rerun of both suites under the bound lock once one exists.

That is wrong as written, and the cause was MY PROBE, not the environment: I called it in a process that had not imported NumPy, so no BLAS library was loaded and there was no pool to report. With NumPy imported first it reports ONE pool - libscipy_openblas, OpenBLAS 0.3.30, pthreads, Haswell - at num_threads = 1, and the control without the NumPy import still returns [], which is what identifies the cause. The earlier documents are NOT edited; the correction lives in the acceptance record and in evidence/thread_policy_observation.json.
the_three_thread_statements_that_must_never_be_reported_as_one_another: single-threaded execution is REQUESTED by the four environment variables, and that is all the variables establish; the loaded BLAS pool is OBSERVED at num_threads = 1 by threadpoolctl after NumPy import; and single-threaded execution at RUNTIME is NOT measured, because neither of those counts executed threads and Python-level thread counting cannot see native OpenBLAS workers.
the_LoCoMo_selection_residual_is_explained_and_the_two_claims_stay_apart: CLAIM 1, the mapping is verified - every one of the 1535 bound ids resolves to a real question in the conversation its id names, and the raw file's structure leaves exactly one consistent assignment out of 3,628,800. the_specific_residual: locomo_6_qa11 carries a correction with error_type TEMPORAL_ERROR whose correct_evidence field is PRESENT BUT EMPTY, so its gold set is empty and step 3 drops it. Its raw evidence resolving fine is exactly why a two-step reconstruction could not see it. Verified end to end read-only: all three steps applied to the verified locomo10.json plus the 156 corrections reproduce the bound cohort EXACTLY - 1535 predicted, 1535 bound, zero on either side - and the corrections file loaded 156 records, matching the frozen constant. No new corpus scan beyond this check, nothing fitted or run, and no question, answer or dialogue text emitted.
the_cohort_is_NOT_changed_and_the_ingestion_deliberately_does_not_implement_that_rule: a reimplementation would be a SECOND selection rule that could drift from the bound cohort, most plausibly by omitting the audit-corrections step - which is exactly the step my reconstruction missed. The ingestion takes the bound ids and resolves each one individually; the test suite asserts that the category-5 question in its fake corpus is not ingested and is reported as present-but-outside-the-cohort instead. The note is documentation, not a dependency: the ingestion never reads audit/errors_conv_*.json.
the_corpus_ingestion_is_WRITTEN_and_has_never_been_EXECUTED_on_a_real_corpus: source byte identity first - streamed sha256 and byte size against bound values BEFORE any parse, so a wrong, truncated or altered file is refused before its content is read; then the bound manifest through the runner's hash-checked loader; then EVERY bound question id resolved individually, refusing missing, extra, misrouted, duplicated and position-mismatched ids rather than repairing them; then archive units and query texts assembled in memory with their gold row indices. It stops there. It does not fit a representation, retrieve, rank, evaluate, bootstrap or seal, and the suite asserts that TfidfVectorizer, TruncatedSVD, sklearn, haar_q and hamming_dist are absent from the module source.
content_never_leaves_memory_and_the_writer_is_the_only_door: text is held in memory and handed to the caller, never printed, logged, placed in an exception message or written. write_ingest_manifest is the only writer - closed schema, then a conservative content policy that refuses any string over 120 characters and any type outside short strings, numbers, booleans, None and containers of those, then the core's refuse-to-overwrite path.
the_gate_stays_shut_and_import_cannot_open_it: core.require_real_data_authorization() is the first statement of every corpus-touching entry point and refuses by default. Importing the module opens nothing - no module-level file access, no path resolved at import time, and no corpus path stored anywhere in the module - so TEST DISCOVERY CANNOT TRIGGER CORPUS ACCESS. Each of these is asserted by a check, not merely stated in prose.
evidence_all_in_the_ACCEPTED_environment: 55 ingestion checks on FAKE FILES ONLY, 85 runner checks and 99 core regression checks, all ALL PASS, under Python 3.13.15 with numpy 2.3.5, scipy 1.17.0, scikit-learn 1.8.0 and pandas 2.2.3, four thread variables at 1 and PYTHONHASHSEED=0. It FAILS CLOSED, so it blocks rather than corrupts. This is the same CRLF hazard that produced false alarms for three earlier auditors, arriving this time as a real defect rather than a false one.
D_5_an_overstatement_of_mine_quoted_byte_verbatim: corpus_ingest.py lines 35-36 say "It is never printed, logged, put in an exception message, or written to disk." That is FALSE. The content policy is applied only inside write_ingest_manifest and never to exception text. _describe() dumps whole objects unbounded - a record dict leaked question, answer and session text in a single 482-character message - and assert_content_free itself interpolates a sub-120-character dict KEY verbatim into its own error. Sub-120 fragments escape through SEVEN exception paths. The 120-character limit was never a guarantee and my prose treated it as one.
D_6: assert_query_transform_is_inherited(mu, D, mu.copy(), D.copy()) PASSES. np.array_equal is value equality, not the "IDENTICAL objects" my docstring claims, and the assertion is disconnected from what apply_archive_transform actually consumed - it is a self-report. The answer settles the second: the environment lock is EXTENDED and pinned implementations are INSTALLED; reference code is NOT vendored into the repository.

It does NOT supply RaBitQ, and RABITQ96 is half of the declared primary contrast SIGN96 - RABITQ96, so an unnamed RaBitQ source would leave the primary estimand uncomputable - exactly the failure section 12 exists to prevent. Under the decided policy RaBitQ likewise comes from a pinned published implementation rather than from vendored code or from an implementation written here. Which package and which version is an engineering pin, decided and recorded by the implementing session under the L-084 consultation threshold, not put back to the Head Researcher.
this_supersedes_a_pre_committed_trigger: an unpushed revision had set a trigger to fetch a reference implementation only if a locally written RaBitQ arm underperformed. Under this decision the reference implementation is used from the start, which is both simpler and better evidence: a comparison against our own reimplementation of a competitor is weaker than a comparison against the competitor's own code.

Neither the string faiss nor 1.13 occurs anywhere in the pushed preregistration at d2cfbaa; that version came from a revision that was never pushed. I relayed an unverified figure into a question to the principal. This is the third appearance in three days of the programme's recurring failure - asserting without checking - and it is recorded here so the count is honest. The decision is bound to the POLICY, which is what the Head Researcher actually answered, and not to that version string.

### L-087

```text
timestamp_utc: 2026-09-11T09:30:00Z
actor_role: Continuity Lead / co-chair (sole writer on main)
predecessor_commit_or_tag: L-086 / 7e1de3a
scope: Reproduce the twelve-byte budget finding independently, and commission a Muse audit of it by writing the task file. No experiment, no seal, no pilot, no run, no corpus access, no edit to the draft branch. Task 4F1 untouched.
changed_or_created_paths: prompts/V52_TWELVE_BYTE_BUDGET_MUSE_AUDIT_TASK_2026-09-11.md with its sha256 sidecar (cf72402e467eca5e00e12e4f9f9233ee59bd72060561d34b82f48f757ed44781); docs/CONTINUITY_LEDGER.md; ops/CURRENT_STATE.json
the_finding_reproduced_here_not_relayed: faiss-cpu 1.15.0 was installed in a scratch virtualenv and the sizes measured directly. RaBitQuantizer(96).code_size is 20, not 12. RaBitQuantizer(96, METRIC_L2, 2).code_size is 44. ProductQuantizer(96,12,8).code_size is 12. Every figure the parallel session reported matched exactly.
the_decomposition_this_session_added: the overhead is FIXED AT 8 BYTES independent of d - 32 gives 12, 64 gives 16, 96 gives 20, 128 gives 24 - so it is two float32 scalars per vector, the correction terms RaBitQ's unbiased estimator and error bound are built on. They cannot be dropped without the method ceasing to be RaBitQ. The single number that follows: the largest d fitting 12 bytes in total at one bit per dimension is 32.
why_this_is_not_bookkeeping: the preregistration's title and arm table equate 96 bit with 12 byte. Arm 4 is the declared primary comparator. Run as written, a 20-byte RaBitQ would be compared against a 12-byte SIGN96 and the result would establish nothing, whichever way it fell. The experiment's central premise does not hold as the document stands.
the_silent_trap_reproduced: assigning nb_bits after construction is silently ignored - RaBitQuantizer(96) then q.nb_bits=2 leaves code_size at 20, a one-bit code, while the constructor form gives 44. A runner written the first way would run arm 5 as an undetected duplicate of arm 4. The remedy proposed is a preregistered abort: every arm asserts its code_size equals its declared budget before computing.
recommendation_put_to_the_head_researcher: hold the budget at twelve bytes TOTAL including every per-vector auxiliary byte, let each method pick its own configuration so RaBitQ runs at d=32, drop arm 5 from the matched-budget contrast because a twelve-byte extended RaBitQ is not meaningfully extended, and report a recall-versus-actual-bytes curve beside the contrast so the compression of RaBitQ to a third of its dimension is visible rather than hidden.
a_fourth_instance_of_my_own_recurring_error: L-086 states that faiss-cpu does not supply RaBitQ. It does; I used it. The policy that entry records - install pinned, do not vendor - is unaffected, and one package rather than two now suffices. The correction is additive here; L-086 is not rewritten.
the_audit_is_commissioned_but_not_run_here: Muse is not present in this environment, which was checked rather than assumed. The task file is written instead, scoped to twelve claims with an exact ref for each and discovery forbidden, because an earlier twenty-one-claim run over eighty-five branches exhausted its search budget and timed out. It carries the flag combination that made the third run stream, the warning that the wrapper captures only the final message, and three questions that matter more than the grades - whether d=32 RaBitQ is a fair comparator at all, whether other arms carry hidden auxiliary cost, and whether a cheaper measurement would settle the same question.
outcome_boundary: Unchanged. Task 4F1 scientific preregistration SEALED at Seal V3; run BLOCKED; production authorization NONE; retrieval-quality outcome access FORBIDDEN. No corpus read, no benchmark retrieval computed, no Task 4F1 artifact touched. The faiss measurements used synthetic dimensions only and no data of any kind.
status: PASS
next_single_action: The budget question stands with the Head Researcher - twelve bytes total, or a budget raised to what every method can reach. Once answered, section 5's seed scope, the budget definition and the code_size abort are corrected together in one revision producing one digest, rather than in three rounds.

### L-088

```text
timestamp_utc: 2026-09-11T18:40:00Z
actor_role: Continuity Lead / co-chair (sole writer on main)
predecessor_commit_or_tag: L-087 / 489e5f9
scope: Record the Head Researcher's decision on what "twelve bytes" means, and the two open items this session closed with published evidence. Recording and verification only. No experiment, no seal, no pilot, no run, no corpus access, no edit to the preregistration draft. Task 4F1 untouched.
changed_or_created_paths: ops/CURRENT_STATE.json; docs/CONTINUITY_LEDGER.md. The decision artifact is on hr/twelve-byte-budget-decision-2026-09-11 at 89d3169adb65a9a2ab7f289997d7c49eb8ccf25c and is NOT merged into main. The evidence is on evidence/twelve-byte-cost-2026-09-11 at 8217704700d793862a6c43130b88236da551f010, also not merged.
the_decision: twelve bytes means at most twelve MARGINAL PERSISTENT BYTES PER VECTOR. Everything written once per stored memory counts - the code, any norm or scale, any correction scalar, any per-vector metadata. Shared model state - codebooks, the OPQ rotation, centroid tables - sits outside the cap but is ALWAYS reported separately in bytes. The preregistration will state that it tests ARCHIVE-LOCAL FITTING and show per-archive state cost separately; a cross-archive or globally shared codebook is a different deployment configuration whose properties may not be presented as a result of this experiment. Artifact V52_TWELVE_BYTE_BUDGET_HR_DECISION_2026-09-11.md, sha256 1832f4b368024edba4a7f5d2d3f925e9c9b1c53367f1a4ed54b7586b2ec8cc26.
why_this_is_not_a_wording_change: RaBitQ at d=96 costs twenty bytes per vector, measured and published at f1149831 and re-measured here. The declared primary contrast SIGN96 minus RABITQ96 therefore compares twelve bytes against twenty and is CANCELLED as written. RaBitQ re-enters only with a configuration that genuinely satisfies the cap; d=32 at one bit is the natural candidate on present measurements. Extended RaBitQ at forty-four bytes cannot enter the twelve-byte race at all. PQ at m=12 by 8 bits does fit the marginal cap, and where fitting is archive-local its very large effective cost on small archives is not to be hidden.
the_abort_the_decision_mandates: the runner asserts measured_persistent_bytes_per_vector at most twelve before computing anything, and aborts when the library's actual code_size or serialization disagrees with the declaration. This exists for a failure already measured rather than imagined: assigning nb_bits after construction is silently ignored, which would have run the extended arm as an undetected duplicate of the one-bit arm.
bound_in_the_same_revision_so_the_bytes_change_once: Recall@100 primary; Recall@3, 10 and 1000 secondary; at least twenty seeds for the Haar and within-96 arms; ten thousand paired bootstrap replicates; gold-evidence cardinality strata; the code_size abort; archive-local state accounting.
two_open_items_this_session_closed_with_published_evidence: first, the SIMHASH control 0.38271667 is NOT sourceless. Two reviewers graded it as lacking provenance because the literal occurs nowhere else, and both were right about the string and wrong about the provenance - the mean of the five per-seed values published at audit_v52_t4c3/AUDIT_REPORT.md lines 88 to 93, sha256 8f6b3105, rounds to exactly that figure at eight decimals. The remedy is to cite the derivation with its artifact digest, seed set and rounding rule, not to hunt for a source that already exists. Second, C4's interpretation half was graded UNVERIFIABLE by both the commissioned auditor and this session because neither read the C++ source; it is settled FUNCTIONALLY instead. Encoding vectors whose norms differ by two orders of magnitude and decoding them shows the decoded magnitudes tracking the input scale, which a sign-only code cannot do, so the eight bytes carry per-vector scale that reconstruction consumes and are not padding.
the_epistemic_limit_kept_rather_than_rounded_up: the functional result does NOT establish that those bytes are specifically the correction terms of RaBitQ's unbiased estimator and its error bound. That attribution needs the paper or the C++ source and stays ungraded. The decision artifact carries the same restriction in its own words.
a_numbering_note_so_two_sessions_do_not_collide: a parallel session prepared a Muse and PQ disposition that was never pushed to canonical main - its commits ec13c81 and 27f50ee exist only in that session's local clone, and its draft artifact sits on hr/l088-muse-pq-disposition-2026-09-11 at 4d6b947b. That disposition is NOT recorded by this entry and takes the NEXT ledger number when its independent runtime replay gate closes. Nothing of it is claimed here.
what_this_does_not_do: it authorizes no execution, seal, pilot or run; grants no outcome access; and disposes of none of G-2's content, L-081's seven findings and nine named test gaps, or integration obligations 4 and 5. The preregistration draft at d2cfbaa was not edited by this session.
outcome_boundary: Unchanged. Task 4F1 scientific preregistration SEALED at Seal V3; run BLOCKED; production authorization NONE; retrieval-quality outcome access FORBIDDEN. No corpus read, no gold read, no benchmark retrieval computed; the faiss measurements used synthetic arrays and one committed metric CSV's archive-cardinality column.
status: PASS
next_single_action: ONE decision remains with the Head Researcher: grant G-3, the authority to write and fix code, or withhold it. That single grant unblocks L-081's seven findings, its nine named test gaps, and integration obligations 4 and 5, all of which are currently frozen for want of code authority; G-2's content can ride with it. Independently of that, the preregistration revision is now UNBLOCKED and may be written to the budget decision recorded here. Task 4F1 remains BLOCKED; outcome access FORBIDDEN.
```

```text
timestamp_utc: 2026-09-16T07:37:21.689849Z
actor_role: Continuity Lead preparing; Head Researcher approved the correction on 2026-09-16 and assigned this number
predecessor_commit_or_tag: L-095 / 5ec3db60
numbering: L-096 is NOT skipped by accident. It is carried by the unmerged branch hr/consolidation-l096-2026-09-14 (907750f) and has not landed on main. The Head Researcher assigned 097 to this entry; if L-096 lands later the sequence closes.
scope: A correction of INTERPRETATION only. No frozen number is disputed, no sealed byte is touched, no Task 4F1 / BEAM surface is run or read. The frozen G = +10.037943 pp is arithmetically correct and stands.
finding: The programme headline "SIGN96 beats FLOAT by about +10 pp" is measured against FLOAT96_UNCENTERED, an unstandardized reference. Standardizing the same float reverses the sign of the comparison. On hit@10 - a metric added by the 2026-09-15 local pilot, not the frozen task's FR@3 - SIGN96 is BEHIND standardized float on every benchmark family measured.
numbers_hit10_pct: LongMemEval sym 86.08 / float 82.55 / float_std 88.30. PerLTQA en_v2 sym 75.76 / float 80.81 / float_std 83.96. RealTalk sym 46.68 / float 36.60 / float_std 48.51. LoCoMo sym 43.84 / float 34.66 / float_std 50.03. sym minus float_std: -2.22, -8.20, -1.83, -6.19 pp.
narrowing: float_std is NOT universally the strongest reference. On two further PerLTQA releases raw float wins: en_v1 float 91.16 vs float_std 89.48, and zh float 82.22 vs float_std 77.90. The correct statement is that the comparison depends on the reference chosen, not that standardized float is best everywhere.
cost_caveat: float_std costs 768 B/doc against 12 B for SIGN96, i.e. 64x. It is a CEILING, not a competitor at the byte budget. Nothing here says the 12-byte code should be replaced. What it says is that "sign quantization beats float" holds only against a weak reference, and that every round built on the old headline inherits the error.
metric_caveat: These are hit@k numbers from a local exploratory pilot carrying [LOCAL EXPLORATORY PILOT] [NOT PREREGISTERED] [NOT FOR CITATION] [DISCLOSE-BEFORE-USE]. The frozen tasks measure expected fractional R@3. This entry corrects how the comparison is FRAMED; it is not a re-measurement of any frozen estimand and does not license reading any frozen outcome.
evidence: branch findings/top10-comparison-2026-09-15, directory research_twelve_byte_pilot_2026_09_15/. HANDOFF_2026-09-15.md sections 6a and 12a; results/HIT10.json, results/LOCOMO.json, results/PERLTQA_en_v1.json, results/PERLTQA_zh.json. Every script self-checks frac@3 against lib_b8.exact_frac on every arm and every query before reporting. Authored body and sidecar: docs/v52/V52_STANDARDIZED_FLOAT_CORRECTION_2026-09-16.md, sha256 2307d5734003e04b1d8441406d1114e45fbf474092b5bdf01f0efa2048297695, prepared on hr/standardized-float-correction-2026-09-16 (7501991).
related_correction: The same pilot resolved the LoCoMo anchor gap. The historical exact_exp and lib_b8.exact_frac are the same estimator (max per-query difference 0.000e+00 over 1535 queries); the 0.136 pp gap is entirely the gold convention, theory_benchmark_test_v1/locomo/run_locomo.py:189 using correct_evidence (the 156 audited corrections) where the local caches hold raw_evidence. That gap is therefore a direct readout of the still-open Head-Researcher obligation, not a metric defect.
disposition: RECORDED AS A FRAMING CORRECTION. No arm winner is declared, no representation change is authorized, no benchmark is pooled, no frozen number is changed.
next: Before any future round quotes the +10 pp gap it must state which float reference it is measured against. Separately unresolved and sharpened by the pilot: binarising the QUERY costs 4.43 pp on PerLTQA and is beneficial on RealTalk, and the cause is unknown.
outcome_boundary: Task 4F1 SEALED / RUN BLOCKED / NO AUTHORIZATION / OUTCOME ACCESS FORBIDDEN. Unchanged by this entry.
